### Page 1

[Table_RightTitle] 
宏观研究|宏观跟踪报告

[Table_Title] 
上下游利润分化持续，企业库存累积

[Table_ReportType] 
——10月工业企业利润数据点评

[Table_ReportDate] 2026年11月27日

[Table_Summary] ⚫ 企业利润修复上行，营收同比小幅增长。10月规上工业企业利
润累计同比上行 0.5个百分点至 16%，当月同比增速由上月的
14.4%上行为 20.1%，营业收入累计同比由 6.6%上行至 6.7%。
从不同类型企业来看，股份制企业、私企利润修复上行，而私
企每百元资产实现的营业收入下滑，国企利润小幅回落，外企
利润持续承压，企业杠杆率整体持平。 
⚫ 营收利润率同环比增长，工业品价格延续上行，工业生产增速
季节性回落。PPI同比由 5%上行至5.7%，原油价格上涨带来的
输入性通胀压力持续。工业增加值同比由5.5%回调至 4.8%，随
十一、中秋节前“抢生产”效应结束，工业生产季节性回落。
营业收入利润率累计值由上月的 5.68%上行至 5.69%，同比上
涨0.44个百分点。 
⚫ 企业成本小幅上行，费用端小幅回落，应收账款扩张压力持续。
每百元资产实现的营业收入持平，企业资产使用效率仍高。资
源品价格高位，企业成本刚性问题延续，成本小幅上行。企业
产成品周转边际放缓，库存水平较高。应收账款同比持续走高，
平均回收期环比上行，企业资金周转效率承压。 
⚫ 采矿业利润保持高位上行，制造业利润分化格局持续。煤炭、
石油和天然气开采依然为采矿业主要拉动项，黑色金属、非金
属矿采选业拖累作用较大。制造业营收、成本和利润率同比均
小幅上行。10月原油价格持续走高，能化产业链利润上行。黑
色金属加工制造主要受低基数影响，跌幅显著收窄；有色金属
加工行业利润受制于价格变动。中游设备制造表现分化，出口
强劲带来支撑。下游制造业利润整体放缓，运输设备制造业、
汽车制造业利润累计同比持续下滑。计算机、通信和其他电子
设备制造业利润虽然放缓，但累计同比依然达到100.9%。消费
品制造业利润增速整体回落。 
⚫ 价格和结构因素带动行业补库，需求和价格制约下游行业利润
走势。工业企业产成品存货累计同比由 11.6%继续上行至 12%，
电子、化学原料等行业在价格和需求等因素下库存增加。本轮
主动补库存周期价格驱动作用显著，内需修复偏慢给营收增速
带来约束。10月大宗商品价格延续高位，上、下游企业价格分
化的格局持续。11月原油价格已由高点显著回落，铜等有色金
属在流动性冲击下价格下跌，中期来看，上下游利润分化有望
边际收敛。未来仍需关注 AI产业链需求放缓、价格变动和行业
利润高基数等带来的影响。地缘政治扰动短期减弱，但上游能
源产品行业价格仍有支撑。 
⚫ 风险因素：地缘政治风险发展超预期，地产下滑幅度超预期，
政策推进和落地不及预期。

万联证券研究所 www.wlzq.cn 第 3 页 共 7 页  
事件 
2026年1-10月，全国规模以上工业企业实现利润总额6.74万亿元，累计同比较上月上
行0.5个百分点至16%，10月当月同比增速由上月的14.4%上行为20.1%。

1    企业利润修复上行，工业品价格保持增长 
企业利润同比修复上行，营收同比小幅增长，营收利润率同环比上涨。2026年10月，
全国规模以上工业企业利润累计同比由9月的15.5%反弹上行至16%，10月当月同比增
速为20.1%，前值为14.4%。规模以上企业营业收入累计同比由6.6%上行至6.7%，营业
收入利润率累计值由上月的5.68%上行至5.69%，环比季节性上行，同比上涨0.44个百
分点。 
工业品价格高位继续上行，工业生产增速季节性回落。10月，PPI同比由5%上行至5.7%，
累计同比由2.3%上行至2.7%，原油价格上涨带来的输入性通胀压力持续，CPI同比延
续低位，上下游价格传导不畅，上下游企业利润分化格局持续。工业增加值同比由5.5%
回调至4.8%，随十一、中秋节前“抢生产”效应结束，工业生产季节性回落。

图表1：工业企业利润当月同比增速持续上行（%） 图表2：拆分看，工业品价格持续上行（%）

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

企业成本小幅上行，费用端小幅回落，应收账款扩张压力持续。10月，企业每百元资
产实现的营业收入持平于上月的73.1元，企业资产使用效率仍高。企业每百元营业收
入中的成本上涨0.04元至85.06元，资源品价格高位，企业成本刚性问题延续。企业
每百元营业收入中的费用由8.35元小幅回落为8.34元，费用压力边际缓解。产成品周
转天数由21天上行至21.2天，企业周转边际放缓，库存水平较高。规模以上工业企业
应收账款同比由9月的10%继续上行至10.5%，应收账款平均回收期环比由71.6天上行
至72.2天，较去年同期的69.8天上行，企业应收账款规模扩张的压力依然较大。部分
企业利用优势地位，挤占上下游供应商和客户利益带来部分影响。

股份制企业、私企利润修复上行，而私企营收边际回落，外企利润持续承压，企业杠
杆率整体持平。2026年10月，分不同企业类型来看，国有企业利润累计同比增速由上
月的13.1%小幅回落至13%，每百元资产实现的营业收入由54.3元小幅回落至54.2元。
外资企业利润累计同比增速由0.5%继续下滑至0.2%。股份制企业利润累计同比由20.8%
小幅上行至21.4%，私营企业由8.7%上行至11.4%，上行幅度较大，而私企每百元资产
实现的营业收入下滑。工业企业资产负债率持平于上月的58.6%，国企、股份制企业、
外企资产负债率与上月持平，仅私企资产负债率小幅回落。

图表5：股份制企业、私企利润上行（%） 图表6：仅私企资产负债率小幅回落(%)

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

2    制造业修复上行，利润分化格局持续 
采矿业利润增速高位继续上行，制造业利润增速边际修复上行，电热气生产和供应业
跌幅进一步加深。三大工业行业来看，采矿业利润累计同比增速由38.2%继续上行至
38.8%，拉动作用持续，采矿业营业收入累计同比由9.6%上行至10.4%，上行幅度相对
最大，而营业成本也上行幅度相对最大。制造业利润总额累计同比由17.0%反弹上行
至17.5%，高技术制造业利润累计同比由52.0%回落至50.9%，制造业营业收入、营业
成本和营收利润率均小幅上行。电力、热力、燃气及水的生产和供应业利润增速由-

14.3%下跌至-14.8%，营业收入同比持平于上月的0.9%。 
煤炭、石油和天然气开采为采矿业拉动持续，黑色金属、非金属矿采选业拖累作用较
大。采矿业内部行业利润表现分化，石油、天然气和煤炭等能源价格10月持续走高，
石油和天然气开采业利润增速持续上行。煤炭开采和洗选业利润累计同比增速上行
4.4个百分点至61.4%，煤炭冬季补库需求提升，价格上行叠加行业供需格局改善，带
动行业利润高增。有色金属矿采选业增速高位持续回落，下行斜率走陡，有色金属价
格承压，叠加成本上升挤压行业利润。黑色金属矿采选业增速今年3月以来持续回落，
非金属矿采选业跌幅进一步加大，下游地产开工偏弱、基建投资放缓，给上游行业带
来拖累。

图表7：采矿业利润增速高位继续上行（%） 图表8：采矿业中黑色、非金属矿跌幅加深（%）

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

能化产业链利润上行，黑色金属主要受低基数影响，有色金属行业利润受制于价格变
动。制造业上游细分行业表现分化，能化产业链来看，10月原油价格持续走高，化学
纤维制造业反弹上行，增速延续高位，纺织服装、服饰业跌幅收窄；化学原料及化学
制品制造业增速也有所上行。黑色金属冶炼及压延加工业跌幅显著收窄，10月利润大
幅放量和低基数为跌幅收窄主要原因，企业供给持续收缩，价格边际改善也带来一定
拉动。非金属矿物制品增速小幅上行，地产链持续低迷对行业拖累显著。有色金属冶
炼及压延加工业利润同比高位持续回落，有色金属价格高位放缓叠加高基数效应为
主要影响。 
下游制造业和消费品制造业增速放缓，中游设备制造业利润分化。中游设备制造业
中，电气机械及器材制造业、专用设备制造业利润跌幅加深，通用设备制造业增速回
升，出口高景气给中游制造业形成支撑。下游制造业利润整体回落，计算机、通信和
其他电子设备制造业利润累计同比由103.8%下行至100.9%，增速依然显著领跑整体，
AI高速发展下行业景气度预期仍高；铁路、船舶、航空航天和其他运输设备制造业增
速由10%回落至9.1%，主要受订单交付节奏扰动；汽车制造业由-22.8%下跌至-26%，
销售走弱和行业价格竞争激烈挤压了利润空间。消费品制造业整体回落，酒、饮料和
精制茶制造、食品制造、农副食品加工、家具制造、文娱用品制造业利润同比跌幅加
深，烟草制品、造纸、医药业利润回落。

3    企业库存持续累积 
价格和结构因素带动行业补库，需求和价格制约下游行业利润走势。工业企业产成品
存货累计同比由11.6%继续上行至12%，库存持续累积，电子、化学原料等行业在价格
和需求等因素下库存增加。工业企业营业收入累计同比由6.6%上行至6.7%，本轮主动
补库存周期价格驱动作用显著，内需修复偏慢给营收增速带来约束。10月大宗商品价
格延续高位，上、下游企业价格分化的格局持续。11月原油价格已由高点显著回落，
铜等有色金属在流动性冲击下价格下跌，中期来看，上下游利润分化有望边际收敛。
未来仍需关注AI产业链需求放缓、价格变动和行业利润高基数等带来的影响。地缘政
治扰动短期减弱，但上游能源产品行业价格仍有支撑。 
图表11：工业企业库存持续上行（%） 图表12：美国、欧洲经济政策不确定性指数小幅回落

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 美國期中選舉由民主黨贏得參議院及眾議院多數席次，川普剩餘兩年任內將面
臨分立政府的局面，市場擔憂民主黨未來傾向加強 AI監管，恐削弱美國經濟成
長動能。
 歐元區 10月 CPI年增率自前月的 3.8%上升至 4%，創下三年來的新高，通膨面
臨上行風險。歐洲央行管委暨斯洛伐克央行總裁卡日米爾(Peter Kazimir)表示，
歐洲央行將持續升息，以防止高通膨根深蒂固；德國央行總裁納格爾(Joachim
Nagel)亦表示通膨前景尚未改善，歐洲央行應於 12 月升息。多位歐洲央行官員
發表偏鷹言論，市場預期歐洲央行未來一年內將再升息二至三次，升息預期升
溫，預計將支撐歐元走勢。
 美國 10 月 ISM 製造業 PMI 為 55.5，高於市場預期，製造業擴張速度加快；另
10 月 ISM 非製造業 PMI為 54.5，持穩於景氣榮枯線之上，顯示美國整體基本面
佳，預計將為美元提供支撐。

 美國 10 月非農就業人數增加 16.5 萬人，優於市場預期的 6.5 萬人，另 10 月失
業率亦由前月的 4.2%降至 4.1%，顯示勞動市場仍具韌性，在美國經濟穩健及通
膨維持高位的情況下，強化聯準會 12月升息預期。
 澳洲央行於 11/3 將現金基準利率調升一碼至 4.85%，係今年以來第五次升息並
創下 2008 年 11 月以來新高水準。然而，此次九名委員中有三人主張維持利率
不變，投票結果出現分歧，加上貨幣政策委員會聲明中，刪除有關可能進一步升
息的明確表述，令市場預期澳洲央行將於 12月暫停升息。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.500，低點在 33.040，以 32.768 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 2 角 9 分。 
    上週台幣走貶，週初，台股開低，市場對 AI投資支出及科技股估值的疑慮，致
使外資連續多日賣超台股並匯出，台幣連貶數日，加以週間美國期中選舉結果出爐，
民主黨勝選，市場擔憂 AI產業監管趨嚴，台股重挫，外資上週累計賣超台股 5,244
億，外資強勢匯出之下，儘管逢高有出口商拋匯抑制台幣貶值速度，台幣仍貶破 33
整數大關，一度觸及年內低點 33.040，惟週五午盤過後，外資及壽險轉向匯入，加
以出口商拋匯需求，台幣跌幅收斂，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.768。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。就國際市場而言，美國非農就業數據意外高於預期、
通膨壓力仍在，強化市場對於聯準會 12月升息預期，高利率將有助國際美元走升；
就台灣市場來看，市場對 AI產業資本支出、融資成本及信用風險產生疑慮，加以民
主黨勝選，引發市場對 AI監管政策收緊與資料中心建設放緩的擔憂，外資持續賣超
台股，令台幣持續面臨貶值的壓力，惟出口商拋匯需求待消化，將抑制台幣貶值速
度，綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.600-33.200

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣先升後貶。週初，國際美元偏弱震盪，人民幣受益走升，境內人民
幣相隔四年多首次升破 6.65整數大關；惟週二中國央行宣布調升外匯存款準備金率，
顯示其抑制單邊升值預期的意圖，人民幣大幅走貶；週間，市場持續消化人行政策
影響，人民幣走貶，最低一度貶至 6.6855，不過美國期中選舉結果出爐，市場不確
定性下降，國際油價、美債殖利率及美元指數皆下跌，緩解人民幣的賣壓，惟中國
國有大行逢低持續買進美元，人民幣反彈力道有限，終場境外人民幣收在 6.6736，
週線貶值 212 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6763，週線貶值 203 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣區間震盪。就國際市場來看，美國非農就業人數新增 16.5 萬
人，就業率下降至 4.1%，勞動力市場意外強勁，強化市場對於聯準會 12 月再次升
息的預期，利差因素支撐美元指數反彈，將抑制人民幣走升；就中國國內市場來看，
人行最新政策顯示其抑制人民幣單邊升值的意圖，惟第四季為結匯旺季，出口商實
質拋匯需求部分緩解人民幣貶值壓力；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，
USD/CNH重返五日均線之上，顯示人民幣強勢升值的趨勢暫歇，綜合以上，預估本
週人民幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6600-6.6900

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週日圓走弱。週初，市場等待美國期中選舉結果，交投偏謹慎觀望，USD/JPY
在 158 附近波動；週間，美國期中選舉由民主黨取得參眾議院多數席次，政治僵局
擔憂令美元走弱，USD/JPY最低來到 157.39；臨近週末，美國公布就業數據優於市
場預期，USD/JPY止跌回升，終場收在 158.45，週線上漲 100 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本央行於季度展望報告中
表示，日本近期實際利率上升，基礎通膨率已加速升至 2%附近，加上日本央行近期
亦強調政策已進入避免通膨超標為重點的新階段，進一步強化了其鷹派立場，市場
預期日本最快將於 12月再次升息，預計將支撐日圓走升。二、美國 10月 ISM製造
業 PMI 為 55.5，優於市場預期，顯示製造業擴張速度加快；另外，美國公布 10 月
非農就業人數增加 16.5 萬人，優於市場預期的 6.5 萬人，美國經濟強勁、勞動市場
仍具韌性，強化聯準會 12月升息預期，美元持穩，預計將有利 USD/JPY走勢。三、
日本財務大臣片山皋月重申美日聯合匯率聲明仍具效力，將於必要時採取外匯干預
行動，以維護外匯市場有序運作，將限縮 USD/JPY上行空間。四、就技術面而言，
在日線圖中，USD/JPY短中天期均線趨平，近期大致在 157 至 158.50 間盤整，匯價
暫無明確方向性。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：157.00-159.50

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 先上後下。週初，在美伊傳出有望和談消息後，歐元自低點小
幅走升至 1.1237；週間，美國民主黨於期中選舉大獲全勝，川普選舉失利，美元偏
弱震盪；臨近週末，美國公布非農就業人數高於市場預期，歐元自高點回落，終場
收在 1.1143，週線下跌 12點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐元區 10月 CPI年增率自
前月的 3.8%上升至 4%，創下三年來的新高，通膨面臨上行風險。此外，歐洲央行
管委暨斯洛伐克央行總裁卡日米爾(Peter Kazimir)表示，歐洲央行將持續升息，以防
止高通膨根深蒂固，並表示目前主要考量已非是否需採取進一步行動，而是須衡量
應升息幾次方能將通膨拉回 2%目標水準，市場預期歐洲央行未來一年內將再升息
二至三次，升息預期升溫，預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、美國公布 10月非農就業人數
增加 16.5 萬人，優於市場預期的 6.5 萬人，另 10 月失業率亦由前月的 4.2%降至
4.1%，顯示勞動市場仍具韌性，強化了聯準會 12月升息預期，美元再度走升至 102，
預計將限縮歐元走升空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD各天期均線向
下發散，近期匯價多次向上測試 1.1270 均拉回留下長上影線，顯示上方仍有賣壓待
消化。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1030-1.1250

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.11.02-115.11.06 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.825%  
30天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.69% 
公債－10 年期-A15109R 1.885% 
－ 5年期- A15108 1.725%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，受台股重挫影響，令週五資金需求上揚，但提存期初市場
資金充裕，短率持穩於近期區間。 
週二央行標售 364天期定存單 1100億元，加權平均得標利率為 1.577%，低於
市場預期區間 1.59%-1.61%，較上次標售結果下跌 2.5個基本點。 
週五隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.805%~0.825%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.67%~1.69%，利率較上週上揚 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投維持冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109R及 5年期指標券 A15108均
未成交，週五等殖系統參考報價中價分別為 1.885%、1.725%。 
股市承壓帶動避險需求，美債利率下滑，令先前台債偏空氛圍緩解，週三標售
30年期公債得標利率 2.267%，低於市場預期區間 2.27%~2.30%，投標倍數 1.85
倍，較上次 1.55 倍提升，不過考量台灣年底仍有升息可能，將抑制買盤向下
追價意願，預估台債利率區間盤整，預估 5 年券區間為 1.70%~1.75%，10 年
券區間為 1.85%~1.90%。

鴻海(2317 TT) Trading Buy

AI 平台世代交替，2027 展望樂觀，投資建議 Trading Buy。 
投資建議：展望 2027 年，Vera Rubin 將成為主要成長引擎，主
要客戶對於 AI 伺服器的需求維持強勁，因此我們給予鴻海
Trading Buy 的投資建議，目標價 272 元。 
3Q26 營收 3.02 兆元，稅後 EPS 5.51 元：3Q26 營收 3.02 兆元，
QoQ+19%， YoY+46%，毛利率 6.02%， QoQ-0.1ppts，主要受產
品組合及原物料上漲影響導致毛利率下滑；營業利益 1,154.99
億元，QoQ+22%，營業利益成長高於營收及營業毛利，反映生
產進入規模經濟；歸屬母公司淨利 775.24 億元，稅後 EPS 5.51
元。 
10/2026 營收 MoM+12.7%，預估 4Q26 營收 QoQ+11.7%：鴻海
10/2026 營收 1.31 兆元，MoM+12.7%， YoY+45.7%，創單月歷
史新高。展望 4Q26，我們預期伺服器營收將季增雙位數。消費

Rubin 新平台出貨而稀釋，季減 0.10ppts/0.06ppts 來到
5.92%/3.77%，歸屬母公司淨利 899.02 億元，QoQ+15.97%，稅
後 EPS 6.39 元。 
Vera Rubin 接力 GB300，2027 年進入新平台主要貢獻期：管理
層預估 4Q26 Vera Rubin 出貨量將呈高雙位數增長。展望 2027
年，整體訂單並未隨近期市場的擔憂而調整，對於 AI 投資的展
望維持正向，預估 AI 機櫃出貨量將呈高雙位數成長。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：   
環境：鴻海採用 TCFD 框架為基礎，全面盤點與評估氣候相關風險與機會，
並導入 ISO 14001 環境管理系統與 ISO 45001 職業安全衛生管理系統，同時
也推動「低碳供應鏈轉型推動計畫」，協助超過 30 家供應商完成節能專案，
累計減少碳排放約 15,000 公噸 CO₂e。鴻海已有超過 150 家供應商完成碳盤
查並簽署科學基礎減碳目標（SBTi），提升能源使用效率與減碳成效。  
社會：鴻海秉持「以人為本」理念，長期支持教育與青年培訓，設立「三創
育成基金會」，提供新創團隊資金與資源支持，自 2015 年以來已經培育超
過  100 組科技創業團隊，促進創新創業生態系發展，為社會培訓科技人才。
治理：董事會有設立「永續發展委員會」，主管由具備三年以上公開發行公
司財務主管經歷之財務長兼任，該委員自 2023 年起每年召開至少四次會議，
全體委員及相關主管亦於任職期間完成 ESG 專業訓練課程，強化永續治理
能力與資訊揭露透明度。 
公司簡介：  
鴻海精密工業股份有限公司（Hon Hai Precision Industry），成立於 1974 年，
由郭台銘創辦，總部位於新北市土城區。公司早期以模具及電子零組件製造
起家，逐步擴展至電子產品組裝、系統整合及設計製造服務，現為全球最大
電子專業製造服務（EMS）廠商。 
集團業務涵蓋消費智能產品、雲端網路產品、電腦終端產品及元件與其他產
品四大領域，旗下包含工業富聯（FII）、鴻騰精密（FIT）等重要子公司，
並透過全球事業單位及子公司分工，提供零組件、模組至整機系統的垂直整
合服務。集團生產及服務據點遍及全球逾 20 個國家及地區，包括中國、台
灣、印度、越南、墨西哥及美國等，全球員工人數於季節性高峰約達 100 萬
人。 
鴻海主要競爭對手包括廣達、緯創、和碩、仁寶及立訊精密等，依產品領域
有所不同。近年積極布局 AI 伺服器、電動車及半導體等新興業務，其中 AI
伺服器受惠全球雲端服務供應商（CSP）資本支出擴張，成為重要成長動能，
並持續鞏固全球電子製造服務龍頭地位。 
3Q26 營收 3.02 兆元，稅後 EPS 5.51 元： 3Q26 營收 3.02 兆元，QoQ+19%，
YoY+46%，毛利率 6.02%，QoQ-0.1ppts，YoY-0.33ppts，主要受產品組合及
原物料上漲影響導致毛利率下滑；營業利益 1,154.99 億元，QoQ+22%，
YoY+64%，營益率 3.83%， QoQ+0.07ppts， YoY+0.4ppts，營業利益成長高
於營收及營業毛利，顯現隨營運規模擴大，生產進入規模經濟；歸屬母公司
淨利 775.24 億元，QoQ+29%， YoY+34%，稅後 EPS 5.51 元。營業利益、淨
利和 EPS 皆創下歷史單季新高。 
10/2026 營收 MoM+12.7%，預估 4Q26 營收 QoQ+11.7%：鴻海 10/2026 營
收 1.31 兆元，MoM+12.7%， YoY+45.7%，創單月歷史新高，除受惠 GB300
持續放量外，iPhone 新機推出亦帶動營收顯著增長；Vera Rubin 僅小量出
貨。展望 4Q26，我們預期 GB200/300 出貨量季增持平至微幅下滑，Vera 
Rubin 則有雙位數以上的成長，並且由於 Vera Rubin 的 ASP 近乎倍增，預
估伺服器營收將季增雙位數。消費電子方面，我們認為記憶體與處理器成本

3 群益投顧 
上升，將使手機出貨面臨壓力，但產品 ASP 提升及 Apple 穩健的品牌效應
仍將為出貨量提供支撐，因此 4Q26 仍將受惠新機推出與傳統旺季帶來的成
長。相較於消費電子的季節性，GB200/300 放量與產品組合變化對全年營運
的影響更大，因此 2026 年的主要成長引擎仍是伺服器業務的擴張；Vera 
Rubin 則為 2027 年的成長建立基礎。綜上所述，群益預估 4Q26 營收 3.37
兆元，QoQ+11.7%；毛利率/營益率持續受 Vera Rubin 新平台出貨而稀釋，
下滑 0.10ppts/0.06ppts 來到 5.92%/3.77%，歸屬母公司淨利 899.02 億元，
QoQ+15.97%，稅後 EPS 6.39 元。 
雲端網路成為營運主軸，規模經濟支持本業獲利成長： 3Q26 雲端網路產品
占營收 54%，高於 2Q26 的 51%。消費智能、電腦終端及元件與其他產品分
別占 29%、12%及 5%。雲端網路產品受 AI 伺服器及機櫃出貨帶動，季增與
年增均強勁。消費智能產品亦受新品與傳統旺季帶動，季增優於公司原先預
期。據我們所知，伺服器約占雲端網路營收的 85%-90%，其中約 70%來自
AI 伺服器。隨雲端網路占比提高，AI 機櫃的交付時程、平台組合及物料認
列方式，將更直接影響鴻海的營收與獲利率。鴻海 3Q26 毛利率 6.02%，QoQ-
0.1ppts，主要受到整櫃 GPU 伺服器高昂的 BOM 所 稀 釋；營 業 利 益 率 3.83%，
QoQ+0.07ppts，雖然毛利率季減，營益率卻有所改善，反映出營收規模擴大
及營運效率提升的效益。 
Vera Rubin 接力 GB300，2027 年進入新平台主要貢獻期：AI 機櫃的新舊平
台交替是鴻海延續成長的核心動能。鴻海預計 2026 年 AI 伺服器機櫃出貨
較 2025 年倍增以上，且既有 GB 平台需求可能延續至 2027 年。此外，Vera 
Rubin 與 GB 系列同為 NVL72，因此我們預期鴻海將可較平滑的實現世代交
替。管理層預估 4Q26 Vera Rubin 出貨量將呈高雙位數增長。展望 2027 年，
伺服器業務上會新增更多 Neocloud 客戶，但主要客戶仍然是大型 CSP。整
體訂單並未隨近期市場的擔憂而調整，對於 AI 投資的展望維持正向，預估
AI 機櫃出貨量將呈高雙位數成長。 
自製零組件與自動化支撐獲利，高 BOM 仍主導毛利率走勢：鴻海透過垂直
整合提高單一專案可承接的製造內容，管理層表示除規模經濟外，零組件自
製率的提高與自動化生產都將為毛利率及營益率提供支撐。根據我們調查，
在晶片以外的零組件自製率約 50%，布局包括連接器、線材、Midplane、交
換器及散熱相關產品，零組件毛利率高於 15%，部分甚至可達 20%，管理層
亦提出 800G 以上交換器全年營收倍增的展望，這些產品有助鴻海由機櫃組
裝向高速互聯及關鍵元件延伸，並可提升附加價值。然而據我們了解目前鴻
海在 GPU 的大型專案中尚未採用 consignment model，記憶體等元件漲價則
主要透過 cost-plus 模式轉嫁給客戶，進入 Vera Rubin 世代後，由於 ASP 將
近倍增，會進一步造成毛利率稀釋，考慮到 GPU Rack 單價極高，我們預估
2027 年毛利率仍將承壓。 
ASIC 拓展產品組合，物料模式與 ASP 差異使營收比重低於出貨量佔比：據
我們了解，鴻海 ASIC 平台約佔 AI 伺服器營收的 20%，較 1H26 略升，且
ASIC 平台成長速度高於 GPU 平台，除既有客戶外亦有新專案，管理層預估
3Q26 ASIC 伺服器出貨量 YoY+150%，高於 GPU 伺服器 70%的出貨量年增。
我們認為 ASIC 專案增加可擴大鴻海在不同運算平台的參與，減少成長來源
集中於單一 GPU 平台的程度。此外，大部分 ASIC 專案將有望採用
consignment model，有助於緩解 AI 伺服器對於整體毛利率的負面效果，然
而 GPU 平台 ASP 顯著高於 ASIC 平台，因此 ASIC 的營收較難完整反映其

4 群益投顧 
專案及製造規模。我們認為目前 ASIC 專案採用 consignment model 尚不足
以顯著改善 GPU Rack 高 BOM 對整體毛利率的稀釋。 
策略投資三菱電機，電動車可望成為未來另一成長引擎：鴻海透過與三菱電
機的策略結盟深化在電動車領域的發展，雖投資金額小於 AI 相關的支出，
但將持續投入研發，目前已做好充足準備量產，將尋機大規模進入 EV 市場，
可望成為未來的另一成長動能。 
預估 2027 年營收 15.1 兆元，稅後 EPS 24.71 元：我們預估隨 AI 機櫃放量
將提高營收與獲利規模，2027 年營收 15.1 兆元，YoY+37.1%，毛利率 5.65%，
YoY-0.4ppts；營業利益 5,181.73 億元，YoY+25.5%，營益率 3.43%，YoY-
0.3ppts；歸屬母公司淨利 3,478.27 億元，YoY+25.4%，稅後 EPS 24.71 元。
Vera Rubin 由初期出貨轉為全年營收貢獻，加上既有 GB300 需求延續及
ASIC 業務擴張，帶動全年營收顯著增長。公司持續布局台灣、美國、墨西
哥及越南產能，亦有助因應客戶在不同地區的交付需求。 
投資建議：受惠 AI 伺服器的強勁需求，2026 AI 機櫃出貨可望倍數增長。
此外我們預期憑藉 iPhone 的品牌效應，4Q26 智慧型消費電子業務可望維持
過往的傳統出貨旺季。展望 2027 年，Vera Rubin 將成為主要成長引擎，主
要客戶對於 AI 伺服器的需求依舊強勁。關鍵製程自動化及零組件自製率的
提升亦有助提升營運效率，為毛利率及營益率提供部分支撐。考量上述原
因，我們給予鴻海 Trading Buy 的投資建議，目標價 272 元。

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Greater China Technology Hardware

Industry View In-Line
4Q26 iPhone builds unchanged: We maintain our 4Q26 iPhone build forecast at
72M units (+33% q/q, -5% y/y). While builds are expected to increase sequentially,
volumes remain below last year’s level, with softer legacy-model production,
including the iPhone 16/17e, weighing on the outlook. Our 2H26 build forecast for

the new iPhone 18 family remains unchanged at 71M units (-21% y/y), with the y/y
comparison also affected by one fewer SKU launched than last year. Within this, we
continue to expect 7M units of iPhone Duo builds. We will continue to monitor the
production ramp and sell-through data for potential upside or downside to our build
estimates. Our full-year forecast implies CY26 iPhone shipments of 234M units
(+3% y/y), with modest growth for the year despite the softer 4Q y/y outlook.

4Q26 iPad builds unchanged; year-end production outlook remains subdued: We
maintain our 4Q26 iPad build forecast at 10.3M units (-10% q/q, -26% y/y), implying
continued pressure on production volumes into year-end. Our full-year estimate
remains 46.8M units (-14% y/y), with the sharper y/y contraction in 4Q pointing to a
weaker finish to the year.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

ab 23 November 2026
Global Research
Initiation of Coverage
Taiwan Union Technology Corporation
Building streams of growth; Initiate at Buy
Initiate on TW CCL                      - TUC: Attractive Buy on business transition
We initiate TUC with a Buy rating and PT of NT$1,150. The company is one of the
leading suppliers for high-speed low-loss CCL materials with increasing presence more
notably in networking switches, servers, power supply units, and satellites, etc. We

believe these new growth areas will drive 65% revenue CAGR through 2026-2028E;
and anticipate product mix shift toward advanced M7+ materials will drive margin
expansion. We see attractive risk-reward with the stock trading at 6x/4x '27/'28E
consensus PE, vs. historical range of 7-17x, backed by   accelerated earnings expansion.
Enhanced profitability as product mix shifts toward M7/M8+
TUC has relatively less high-end CCL exposure compared to EMC (M7+ was 36% of

2025 sales, vs. 50-55% for EMC) but has a stronger position in high-end networking
switch market with potentially growing exposure in AI ASIC projects. We assume  M7+
CCL product sales contribution to reach 65% by 2030E driven by 1) its expanding
footprint in networking, which is upgrading toward M7/M8+ from 800G/1.6T+; 2)
potential traction in ASIC platforms; 3) power supply related sales contribution  to grow
from 5-10% in H225 to 15%+ in 2027E; and 4) potential opportunities in LEO satellites.

We see differentiated growth engines to drive product mix improvement and margin
expansion. We expect EPS to grow at 89% CAGR through 2026-2028E, outpacing
topline growth of 65% for the same period.
Capacity additions to capture  growth with continued pricing adjustments
Targeting opportunities in the material upgrade cycle fuelled by stronger computing/
networking demand and hence also higher power requirement, TUC is investing for
future growth with capacity expansion planned for Thailand and China. Potential new

CCL entrants currently do not have access to equipment and existing suppliers' capacity
expansion is also constrained by extended equipment lead time (now 2 years+) so overall
industry supply shall remain relatively stable through 2028E. Mid to low-end CCL  pricing
uplifts are expected to increase by at least double digits into H226 with upward trend
sustaining into H127 before new e-glass capacity scales up. M7+ high-end CCL have
started to raise pricing in Q426 to reflect raw material increases, and CCL makers expect

potential further pricing uplifts into 2027E as high-end copper foil and low-DK glass
cloth supply might tighten.
Valuation: Initiate at Buy with PT$1,150
In light of market concerns on AI slowdown, we noted that our estimates are below
consensus factoring in slower capacity ramp (4.6mn/5.2mn in '28/'30E, 10-30% lower
than announced plans) and much less pricing uplifts. Our NT$1,150 PT is based on 8x
'28E PE, which we think is fair at the low-end of historical trading range of 7-17x, and

deem conservative with 36% '26-'30E earnings CAGR, implying only 0.2x PEG.  Despite
more conservatives estimates, we still see good upside on current valuation.

### Page 2

Taiwan Union Technology Corporation UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Is favourable pricing environment and current CCL industry upcycle sustainable?
We continue to see AI CCL content value increases as computing and networking/interconnect
demands surges. Extended equipment lead time and qualification process along with increasing
manufacturing challenges are also suggesting more constrained upstream inputs. We expect

persisting CCL industry shortage to drive better pricing power and believe high-end CCL have
entered a spec-driven structural upgrade cycle, not a temporary commodity uptick.
Q: Could high-end CCL drive the next phase of growth for TUC?
While TUC has relatively less high-end CCL revenue exposure than EMC, we still see multiple drivers
lifting its M7+ product exposure that should lead to better growth visibility and margin profile,
including 1) its expanding footprint in networking, which is upgrading toward M7/M8+ from

800G/1.6T+; 2) potential traction in ASIC platforms; 3)  potential opportunities in LEO satellites. We
expect product mix transition to drive accelerated earnings growth in the next few years.
Among CCL supply chain, we believe TUC is looking to benefit from the structural spec upgrades
cycle, with growing presence in AI server,  not aggressively head-to-head in the hot zones, but actually
seeking growth in a number of diversified areas including networking/switch, power supply, and LEO

satellites, etc. We still see multiple drivers lifting its M7+ product exposure that should lead to better
growth visibility and margin profile.
Top 6 CSP capex spending has revised up another leg, strengthening visibility into AI-related CCL
demand. Hyperscaler capex grew 25% QoQ/ 101% YoY in Q326 to US$252.7bn, with UBS 2026/27E
forecasts revised up 2%/18% to imply +96%/+74% YoY growth to US$897bn/US$1.6tn.
Meanwhile, the pricing backdrop remains supportive,   with Taiwanese CCL suppliers raising prices by

10-30% QoQ in Q126 and Q226, followed by further double-digit increases in August and
September. EMC’s latest commentary reinforces this trend,  noting 5-20% price increases across its
portfolio in Q426F and further upside possible should the supply-demand mismatch persist.
Following its sharp re-rating, TUC has corrected 65% from its recent October peak, versus a 28%
decline in the TAIEX, amid broader tech-sector weakness and profit-taking. The shares trade at 6x/4x

2027E/2028E consensus P/E, with the 6x 2027E multiple at one standard deviation below their
historical 12-month forward average of 12x, offering an attractive entry point. We believe the market
underappreciates TUC’s product-mix and profitability improvement, driven by next-generation CSP
penetration, a richer M8/M9 mix and expanding networking, optical and power exposure, supported
by capacity expansion through 2028. We value TUC at 8x 2028E P/E, supported by 36% 2026–30E

earnings CAGR, implying a 0.2x PEG ratio.

Source: UBS estimates
Taiwan Union Technology (TUC) was founded in 1974, and is a leading global supplier of copper-clad
laminate (CCL), prepreg and mass-lamination services for printed circuit boards. The company

specializes in high-speed, low-loss materials, with products used across telecom infrastructure, high-
speed networking, servers and data centers, automotive electronics and LEO satellite applications. In
2025, ELL/SLL (M7/M8) accounted for 36% of CCL revenue, VLL/LL (M4/M6) 32%, HDI/N-LL 24%
and other products 8%. TUC’s manufacturing footprint spans Taiwan, Mainland China and Thailand.
Pivotal Questions

Company Description

### Page 3

Taiwan Union Technology Corporation UBS Research
Business overview
Founded in 1974 as Taiwan Union Glass Industrial Co., Ltd., TUC initially manufactured
optical glass before pivoting into CCL and prepreg in 1997 and adding mass-lamination
services in 2001.  The company subsequently built its expertise around high-reliability,
low-loss materials, introducing low-loss high-frequency communication laminates in
2008-09 and second-generation very-low-loss products in 2011-15, which established

its presence in 5G base stations and high-end networking equipment.
As network speeds migrated from 100G/400G toward 800G and AI infrastructure, TUC
progressively upgraded its product portfolio from VLL/LL (M4/M6) to higher-end ELL/SLL
(M7/M8) materials for switches, AI servers and CSP platforms, while also extending into
emerging applications such as power, LEO satellites and optical. According to Prismark,
TUC held 16.4% of the global halogen-free high-speed laminate market in 2025,

ranking No. 3 globally. ELL/SLL and VLL/LL products together accounted for 68% of
2025 CCL revenue versus 24% for HDI/N-LL, reflecting its increasing exposure to high-
speed infrastructure applications.

Networking remains a key structural growth driver with 800G/1.6T share gains
TUC was an early supplier to hyperscale networking applications, with one CSP
customer adopting its materials for 100G data-center switches in 2015, followed by two

other major CSPs in 2016. This established networking franchise is becoming
increasingly valuable as switch speeds advance. Having been underrepresented in the
400G generation, TUC is now gaining share in 800G, supported by its relationships with
leading customers in networking/ethernet/switch/router as well as growing penetration
into white-box switch programs. The transition from 400G to 800G and ultimately 1.6T
should continue to drive both share gains and content growth. While 400G primarily

uses M7-class materials, 800G increasingly requires higher-value M8 solutions and 1.6T
uses M8+ solutions alongside greater PCB complexity and rising optical content.
Management noted that recent demand has exceeded the available capacity of several
Korean and Japanese competitors, creating opportunities for TUC to capture
incremental business from customers planning 2027-28 deployments. The company is
also expanding beyond 400G programs into 800G and early 1.6T platforms. Given

networking's superior margins and customers' greater willingness to pay for
performance and secured capacity, we believe TUC is willing to allocate capacity to
800G/1.6T networking products over AI server products.
AI server penetration expands from ODMs to CSP ASICs
TUC initially built its AI server exposure through ODM-led platforms with a focus on
HGX-related boards,  where increasingly open supplier sourcing has supported share
gains. Its opportunity is also expanding as AI server designs migrate from predominantly

M7 toward M8 qualification, raising the value of TUC’s high-speed CCL offering.

### Page 4

Importantly, management views ODM business as particularly attractive given better
pricing and greater influence over supplier selection, and continues to target HGX
opportunities through these customers.
TUC further broadened its exposure into CSP ASIC platforms in 2025 through a major
ASIC customer's project, with penetration continuing on that next-gen project taking
place in 2026. While this represents an important expansion beyond its traditional ODM

base, management noted this ASIC project pricing is less favorable than ODM business,
suggesting the strategic value lies more in expanding TUC’s presence in large-scale ASIC
platforms than in margin uplift. We expect AI server growth to increasingly come from a
combination of deeper ODM penetration and broader CSP ASIC participation.
LEO satellite: OOC capacity positions TUC for further Starlink penetration
LEO satellite build-out is emerging as a key structural growth driver for TUC, supported

by growing exposure to a leading US LEO customer and strategic Thailand capacity. The
customer has accelerated constellation deployment to 11.1k satellites in orbit by Aug-
26, up from c.9.4k when TUC began ramping its LEO business, while end-subscribers
doubled YoY to 12mn, supporting a multi-year demand runway driven by ongoing
constellation expansion and replacement cycles. TUC's current participation remains in
ground-side user terminals, where it emerged as a second source after EMC as the

primary CCL supplier, benefiting from a technology migration from M6-grade TU-883A
to higher-value M7 materials that should drive higher ASPs and gross margins.
Importantly, TUC's Thailand facility has been designated to support the program's out-
of-China (OOC) manufacturing requirements and is well positioned to supply
downstream PCB makers, creating additional share-gain opportunities as capacity
ramps. Beyond user terminals, TUC represents an attractive satellite-side option, where

incumbent CCL suppliers remain Isola and EMC; the company is undergoing
qualification for M8 materials, a step up from the M7 platform used in the prior V3-
generation satellite design. Successful qualification would expand TUC's content
opportunity into higher-value satellite applications and represents upside not yet
embedded in current expectations. We therefore view TUC's LEO growth thesis as a
combination of expanding user-terminal volumes, material upgrades from M6 to M8,

greater utilization of its Thailand OOC capacity, and potential entry into satellite
applications through M8 qualification, positioning the company to capture a growing
share of the rapidly scaling LEO ecosystem.
Strong Positioning in High-Growth Power Applications
Beyond networking and AI servers, power applications have become an increasingly
important growth driver for TUC, accounting for roughly 15% of revenue in Q326, up
from 8% in Q325. The segment, which mainly consists of AI power supply units (PSUs),

but also EV charging and consumer fast-charging applications, has been growing at
nearly 50% YoY, matching the growth rate of the company's M7/M8 business.  UBS
estimates the AI Power TAM will expand from US$18bn in 2026E to US$55bn in 2028E,
supporting demand for power-related CCL as AI infra scales (LINK).
TUC appears particularly well positioned to capture this growth. The company supplies
power-related materials to key customers including one dominant Taiwanese power

supplier that holds ~70% global market share, with Shengyi serving as the primary
second source. Management highlighted that safety and reliability remain critical
considerations, particularly as the industry moves toward higher-voltage architectures,
creating sticky customer relationships and high barriers to entry.
Thicker copper should provide a second growth lever through rising material content
and ASPs. Standard PCBs typically use 1-2oz copper layers, while PCBs with copper

thickness above 3oz (105µm) are generally classified as thick-copper PCBs. Greater
copper thickness reduces electrical resistance and heat generation at a given current,
making it particularly suitable for high-current power applications. Industry
specifications are moving from around 3oz toward 5-6oz as AI power-delivery
requirements increase. TUC has secured thick-copper CCL programs since 2025, and we
expect the combination of higher copper content and more demanding processing

requirements to increase CCL ASPs and material value per board. We view power
applications as an underappreciated growth vector that combines strong market
positioning, structural end-demand and rising content per system.

### Page 5

Key expansion focus on Mainland China and Thailand, with existing capacity in
Taiwan
TUC's manufacturing footprint started in Hsinchu, Taiwan where it started mass
production in 1997 and mass-lamination services from 2001. The company then
expanded into Mainland China, adding its Changshu plant in 2005 and Zhongshan
plant in 2010.
TUC entered 2026 with 2.4mn sheets/month of CCL capacity across Taiwan, Mainland
China and Thailand, following the ramp-up of its first 300k sheets/month in Thailand

end of 2025. A further 300k sheets/month expansion at the Thailand plant began
contributing in July 2026 and is expected to reach full production in Q426, bringing
group capacity to 2.8mn sheets/month by end-2026.
In Mar 2026, TUC announced a new round of capex investment in Changshu and
Thailand, with planned capex of Rmb953mn and Bt6.1bn, respectively. The projects will
add 600k sheets/month at each site, with Changshu expected to commence production

in H127 and Thailand adding 300k sheets/month in each of Q327 and Q427. In Jul
2026, the company announced another expansion cycle for 2028-29, investing
Rmb2.36bn in a new Zhongshan facility for an additional 1.2mn sheets/month and
Bt7.79bn in further Thailand expansion of  additional  750k sheets/month.  We expect
group capacity to reach c.5.35mn sheets/month by end-2028, 5.95mn by end-2029,
and 7.45mn by end-2030  as these projects progressively come online.

Shareholder structure:
Publicly listed on the Taipei Exchange in Dec 2003, TUC was founded by Wen-Bing Hsin
in 1974 and remains controlled by the Hsin family. Current chairman  Chung-Heng Hsin
holds ~3.8% of outstanding shares, while family investment vehicles DingXingFeng Co.
and Chang Yu Hong Co. together hold ~4.7%, bringing total family-related ownership
to ~13%. FINI ownership peaked at ~56% in 2020 amid strong 5G infrastructure

demand, before declining as US restrictions on Huawei disrupted China 5G equipment
production and slowed related CCL demand, followed by 2022-23 electronics inventory
correction. It bottomed at ~21% in 2023 and had recovered to ~39% in 2025/26 on AI/
ASIC re-rating, before easing to ~33% recently (vs Taiex's FINI holding 47%).

Taiwan Union Technology Corporation UBS Research
Financial analysis
Q326 earnings beat on strong GM expansion
Q326 EPS of NT$12.88 came in above consensus of NT$12.10 on stronger sales and
GM. Sales of NT$17.95bn rose 25.5% QoQ and 122.6% YoY, while GM expanded
7.2ppts QoQ to 36.9%, benefiting from price hikes, product mix improvement and the
earlier-than-expected Thailand capacity ramp, which helped the Thailand operation turn
profitable in Q3. Opex increased 19% QoQ, below the 26% QoQ revenue growth,

supporting operating leverage and OPM expansion to 31.0%; management expects the
opex ratio to decline as revenue scales. By technology, M7/M8 accounted for 38% of
sales, down 1ppt QoQ as price hikes were implemented earlier for mid-to-low end CCL
products, although management noted continued shift towards high-end CCL mix, with
M7 to grow faster than M8 in the near term. M4/M6 fell 3ppts to 28% (+ 13% QoQ),
while HDI & non-low-loss expanded 3ppts to 19% (+49% QoQ), and Others, including

Power, gained 1ppt of mix to 15% (+42% QoQ). Management highlighted particularly
strong growth in Power, a high-ASP, high-margin segment where TUC has strong
competitiveness and sees as one of its key development focuses.
Q426 to see continued pricing momentum.
We forecast Q426 sales of NT$21.24bn, up 18% QoQ, supported by another 100k
sheets of Thailand Phase I capacity following the 200k-sheet ramp in Q326, alongside
tight demand across multiple applications.  We expect GM to remain strong at 37.1% as

high-end product price hikes start to take effect in Q426E, reflecting raw-material cost
increases that TUC absorbed in prior quarters. Management expects sub-M7 product
pricing to remain firm into H127F given continued E-glass cloth tightness, while high-
end copper foil and LDK supply could tighten into 2027 and support further price
increases. We forecast Q426E EPS of NT$16.15, up 25% QoQ.

Pricing and capacity ramp drive earnings growth through 2028E
We forecast TUC’s revenue to grow 80%/51% YoY in 2027/2028E, supported by
substantial capacity expansion and broad-based demand across AI servers, networking,
optical modules, satellite and Power applications. Near-term growth should  benefit from

continued price increases, with hikes in lower-end CCL extending into 2027 and higher-
end products beginning to see price increases. Capacity additions in Changshu,
Zhongshan and Thailand support volume growth, with group capacity expected to
increase 31%/39% YoY in 2027/2028E. TUC is also building qualifications for next-
generation CSP platforms, with current efforts focused on M8/M9 opportunities,
though we expect growth to be stepwise as customer programs move through

### Page 7

qualification and ramp-up. We believe networking and optical applications should
provide additional growth, with optical modules requiring at least M7 and potentially
M9 materials, while PCIe 6 adoption supports further general server demand. We also
expect Power to become an increasingly important driver, given its strong growth,
attractive ASP/margin profile and TUC’s competitive positioning in the segment.
We expect earnings growth to outpace revenue growth through 2028E as pricing, richer

product mix and operating leverage compound the benefit from higher volumes. We
forecast GM to increase from 33.5% in 2026E to 38.7%/40.7% in 2027/2028E,
reflecting a greater contribution from M8/M9 and other high-value products, continued
pricing benefits and improving scale. At the operating level, we expect opex to grow
slower than revenue as the business scales, providing a second layer of margin
expansion, with OPM rising from 27.4% in 2026E to 33.1%/35.7% in 2027/2028E. As a

result, we forecast EPS of NT$92.06/NT$149.51 in 2027/2028E, up 121%/62% YoY
versus revenue growth of 80%/51%, respectively, highlighting the accelerating earnings
benefit from both GM expansion and operating leverage.

### Page 8

Sensitivity analysis
Quantifying earnings downside  risks if AI spending slows down from 2028
The US Department of Commerce measures announced on 16 November seek to
prevent China AI-chip controls from being circumvented through third-country data
centers. The rules extend controls beyond the physical shipment of advanced chips to
who ultimately accesses the compute, while introducing licensing requirements for
advanced-chip exports, re-exports and transfers to Singapore, Malaysia, Indonesia and

Thailand, as well as Brazil, which BIS identifies as presenting higher-than-normal
diversion risk. Chips destined for US-headquartered companies or US-parented
subsidiaries are exempt from this country-level requirement, subject to reporting
conditions. The rules do not altogether prohibit ASEAN AI investment, but raise licensing
and compliance hurdles for parts of the data center ecosystem (LINK). This raises
concerns on first-order impacts of likely to be delayed GPU deployment and slower data

center build-outs. While we believe AI data center build-out remains supported by  cloud
computing power growth, enterprise AI and inference, we note it is constrained by data-
sovereignty and latency requirements, as well as power and land constraints elsewhere.
We see market continues to expect strong 2027 but now more cautious for 2028. We
view some market expectations of a down year might be too conservative as UBS team
has been noting that operating cash flow should support sustainable capex for 2028.

That being said, we still see some sensitivity around potential acts might cap
hyperscalers on securing permitted sites and/or receiving power/water permits.
Below we examine the potential downside risks into 2028 earnings if AI spending
materially slows down vs our current base case scenario. Our key assumptions and
findings are as follows:
 If capacity expansion plan falls short of expectations...: The company has
announced plans to expand CCL monthly capacity from 2.41mn sheets in 2025 to

5.35mn/7.45mn sheets by 2028F/2030F. As we take a more conservative stance to
factor in the AI demand uncertainties, our current model assumes its CCL monthly
capacity of 4.59mn sheets in 2028E, below the company's announced plan of
5.35mn sheets;  and note that if capacity additions were only
50%/30%/20%/10% executed compared to our base case scenario, this will
imply potential 2028E earnings downside of 26%/36%/40%/45% to our current
estimates.

 ...and pricing environment becomes less favourable: We currently expect to
see AI CCL content value increases driven by computing and networking/
interconnect demands; and extended equipment lead time and qualification
process along with increasing manufacturing challenges are also suggesting more
constrained upstream inputs. That being said, if pricing power starts to weaken on
AI spending slowdown, assuming pricing falls short of our current estimates by

5%/10%/15%/20%, on top of lowered capacity, this will imply potential earnings
downside of 32%/45%/53%/60% to our current 2028E estimates.

TUC’s 12-month forward P/E historically traded below 15x prior to 2023, before re-

rating with the onset of the AI cycle. The re-rating accelerated from 2025 through 2026,
driven by 1) expectations for CCL price increases amid tightening supply, supporting a
stronger margin outlook; 2) growing confidence in TUC's M8/M8+ positioning and
exposure to hyperscaler ASIC programmes; and 3) capacity expansion, including in
Thailand to support higher-end demand and further product mix improvement.  These
factors lifted its forward P/E to a historical peak of 39x in June 2026. Since the AI cycle

began, TUC has traded within a roughly 10–40x forward P/E range, averaging around
17x.
Following its sharp re-rating, TUC’s share price has corrected 65% from its recent peak
since October, versus TAIEX down 28% amid broader tech-sector weakness and profit-
taking following a strong run. TUC now trades at 7x/4x our 2027E/2028E EPS, with its
12-month forward P/E at one standard deviation below its historical average. Our
earnings forecasts are below Street estimates, reflecting our view that TUC’s greater

exposure to mainstream CCL products could leave it more exposed to pricing pressure
and constrain margin expansion. Nevertheless, we expect a gradual improvement in its
product mix: while M7/M8 represented only 36% of 2025 sales, we expect TUC’s high-
end exposure to increase as networking migrates from 800G toward 1.6T,  and higher-
value power and LEO applications contribute more meaningfully. This mix shift, together
with operating leverage, underpins our forecast for gross margin to expand from 33.5%

in 2026E to 40.7% in 2028E and EPS growth to materially outpace revenue growth.
Although we take a more cautious view than the Street on the extent of this
improvement, we believe the current valuation remains attractive even on our
conservative earnings forecasts.
Our NT$1,150 price target is based on 8x 2028E P/E, toward the lower end of TUC’s
historical 7–17x average forward P/E trading range. Our conservative multiple reflects
broader tech-sector selling, rising concerns over slower AI spending and uncertainty

around PTFE adoption, alongside TUC’s relatively lower high-end CCL exposure. Our
target multiple compares with 12x/10x for EMC/ITEQ. We believe the discount to EMC
appropriately reflects TUC’s product positioning, while its improving earnings profile
supports a premium to the broader CCL and PCB groups, which trade at 8x and 7x
2028E P/E on average, respectively.
TUC  is trading at NT$623.00 (as of 20
Nov 2026)

power applications, and capacity expansion. We assume GM of 40.7%, OPM of 35.7%
and EPS of NT$149.5. We apply 8x 2028E P/E to derive our NT$1,150 base-case
valuation.
DOWNSIDE (NT$375): We assume 2028E sales growth of 21.7% YoY, reflecting slower
AI/CSP demand, delays in M8/M9 qualification and ramp-up, and earlier normalization in
CCL pricing as new E-glass supply comes online. We assume GM of 35.7% and EPS of NT

$75.1, reflecting weaker high-end mix and operating leverage. We apply 5x 2028E P/E to
derive our NT$375 downside valuation.

### Page 13

Taiwan Union Technology Corporation UBS Research
Company Description
Company Description
Taiwan Union Technology (TUC) was founded in 1974 and is a
leading global supplier of copper-clad laminate (CCL), prepreg
and mass-lamination services for printed circuit boards. The
company specializes in high-speed, low-loss materials, with
products used across telecom infrastructure, high-speed
networking, servers and data centers, automotive electronics and

LEO satellite applications. In 2025, ELL/SLL (M7/M8) accounted
for 36% of CCL revenue, followed by VLL/LL (M4/M6) at 32%,
HDI/N-LL at 24% and other products at 8%. TUC's
manufacturing footprint spans Taiwan, mainland China and
Thailand.
Industry Outlook
The CCL industry outlook remains positive, supported by AI
server investment and upgrades to high-speed networking
infrastructure. Larger, higher-layer-count PCBs and tighter signal-
integrity requirements are increasing demand for high-

performance, ultra-low-loss laminates, supporting both material
content growth and a richer product mix. Tight supply of
specialty glass fabric and other inputs is supporting laminate
price increases, although rising material costs and supply
constraints remain risks to margins and shipment growth. These
trends should favour suppliers with advanced material
capabilities and reliable access to critical inputs.

Company Description
Taiwan Union Technology (TUC) was founded in 1974 and is a leading global supplier of
copper-clad laminate (CCL), prepreg and mass-lamination services for printed circuit boards.
The company specializes in high-speed, low-loss materials, with products used across telecom

infrastructure, high-speed networking, servers and data centers, automotive electronics and
LEO satellite applications. In 2025, ELL/SLL (M7/M8) accounted for 36% of CCL revenue,
followed by VLL/LL (M4/M6) at 32%, HDI/N-LL at 24% and other products at 8%. TUC's
manufacturing footprint spans Taiwan, mainland China and Thailand.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value TUC using a PE-based methodology.
Key risks include: 1) slower-than-expected high-end CCL adoption and AI infrastructure

deployments or delays in server upgrade cycles; 2) delays in new capacity expansion and
production ramp-up; 3) stronger-than-expected competition and market-share losses in high-
end CCL and power applications; and 4) higher glass fiber, copper foil and other raw-material
costs and weaker-than-expected cost pass-through to customers.

### Page 17

Quantitative Research Review
UBS Global Research publishes a quantitative assessment of its analysts' responses to certain questions about the likelihood of an occurrence
of a number of short term factors in a product known as the 'Quantitative Research Review'.  The views for this month can be found below.

by the analyst to indicate that the stock's price target and/or rating may change in the near term, usually in response to an event that

may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have a 12-month investment horizon.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

7717.TT 萊德光電-KY 
 太空資料中心不是夢

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
萊德光電股份有限公司成立於 2014 年 8 月，為光纖被動元件廠商。產品包括光纖通
訊與雷射元件、衛星雷射通訊元件、端面檢測儀與設備、智慧照明等。3Q26 萊德光
電-KY 營收組成為衛星雷射通訊被動元件 57%、光纖雷射被動元件 16%及其它 27%。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
區間操作 495 
【前次投資建議】無 
【大盤指數/股價】36033 / 462

➢ 衛星雷射通訊被動元件帶動 3Q26 營收、毛利大幅成長 
萊德光電-KY 3Q26 營收 3.4 億元(YoY+65.4%，QoQ+1.4%)，營收年
增主因為衛星雷射通訊被動元件營收由 3Q25 的 2 億元(營收比重 48%)，
年增 94% 至 3Q26 的 3.4 億元( 營收比重 57%) 。毛利率 68.6% 
(YoY+10.5ppt，QoQ+4.7ppt)，EPS 2.33 元。3Q26 萊德光電-KY 營收
組成為衛星雷射通訊被動元件 57%、光纖雷射被動元件 16%及其它
27%。低軌衛星客戶訂單持續挹注下，預計 4Q26 營收季增 16.6%至 3.9
億元，預估 2026 年營收 13.2 億元(YoY+53.4%)，毛利率 64.1%，EPS 
10.06 元。 
➢ 2027 年 Starship 載運 V3 引領衛星 LCT 需求成長 
研究員推測 2027 年 SpaceX 發射火箭將由 Falcon 9 改為 Starship 為
主，發射次數約 100 次，載運 V3 數量約 6,000 顆，並預期 Blue Origin、
Amazon LEO 將藉由 Starship、ULA 載運 100 顆、300 顆衛星至太空
軌道，載運總數估達 6,400 顆(YoY+51.1%)。在衛星迭代上，SpaceX V3
新增 Direct-to-Call 功能，上行頻寬約 160 至 200Gbps，下行頻寬達
1Tbps，所需光放大器級數上升，帶動衛星雷射通訊被動元件用量由 3 個
提升至 6 個、9 個。目前公司新舊客戶訂單及研發需求正持續增加，假
設光纖雷射被動元件、智慧照明等營收年增 20%，端面檢測儀與製造設
備出貨 10 台。其中，衛星雷射通訊被動元件營收年增約 50%，營收比
重達 60％，預估 2027 年營收 18 億元(YoY+36.1%)，毛利率 64.3%，
EPS 15.35 元。 
➢ 首次評等給予區間操作，目標價 495 元 
萊德光電-KY 於 2021 年切入衛星雷射通訊領域後，目前在太空雷射元
件領域競爭對手甚少。現階段低軌衛星產業處於發展初期，2026 年至
2030 年衛星發射數量隨著火箭發射數量增加、運載能力增強而以雙位數
CAGR 成長，將逐年帶動萊德光電-KY 衛星雷射通訊被動元件等營收走
高。參考近期低軌衛星產業 Forward PE 評價落在 15X~40X 區間。以
2028 年 EPS 換算，目前為 23X，位於區間中緣，故首次評等給予區間
操作，目標價 495 元(2028F PE 25X)。

7717 萊德光電-KY 加權指數

個股報告

公司介紹 
萊德光電股份有限公司成立於 2014 年 8 月，為光纖被動元件廠商，主營運子公司為
設在美國西雅圖的 Lightel Technologies, Inc.。產品包括光纖通訊元件、光纖雷射元
件、衛星雷射通訊元件、光纖端面檢測儀與光纖元件製造設備、智慧照明等。3Q26 萊
德光電-KY 營收組成為衛星雷射通訊被動元件 57%、光纖雷射被動元件 16%及其它
27%。 
圖 1：萊德光電-KY 營收組成

資料來源：國票投顧 
衛星雷射通訊被動元件帶動 3Q26 營收、毛利大幅成長 
萊德光電-KY 3Q26 營收 3.4 億元(YoY+65.4%，QoQ+1.4%)，營收年增主因為衛星
雷射通訊被動元件營收由 3Q25 的 2 億元(營收比重 48%)，年增 94%至 3Q26 的 3.4
億元(營收比重 57%)。毛利率 68.6%(YoY+10.5ppt，QoQ+4.7ppt)，年增 10.5ppt
中，約 4ppt 來自於美國海關退稅款，其餘 6.5ppt 則為高毛利之衛星雷射通訊產品營
收比重提升所帶動。EPS 2.33 元。3Q26 萊德光電-KY 營收組成為衛星雷射通訊被動
元件 57%、光纖雷射被動元件 16%及其它 27%。儘管原本預計在 4Q26 投產之泰國
廠，遞延至 2Q27 開始小量出貨，但在低軌衛星客戶訂單持續挹注下，預計 4Q26 營
收季增 16.6%至 3.9 億元，預估 2026 年營收 13.2 億元(YoY+53.4%)，毛利率 64.1%，
EPS 10.06 元。 
2027 年 Starship 載運 V3 引領衛星 LCT 需求成長 
觀察 2025 年、2026 年(截至 11/20)SpaceX LEO 發射數量各為 3,233 顆、2,300 顆，
火箭發射次數為 170 次、127 次，多以 Falcon 9 運載為主。研究員推測 2027 年 
SpaceX 發射火箭將由 Falcon 9 改為 Starship 為主，發射次數約 100 次，載運 V3 數
量約 6,000 顆，並預期 Blue Origin、Amazon LEO 將藉由 Starship、ULA 載運 100
顆、300 顆衛星至太空軌道，載運總數估達 6,400 顆(YoY+51.1%)。在衛星迭代上，
SpaceX V3 新增 Direct-to-Call 功能，上行頻寬約 160 至 200Gbps，下行頻寬達
1Tbps，所需光放大器級數上升，帶動衛星雷射通訊被動元件用量由 3 個提升至 6 個、
9 個。目前公司新舊客戶訂單及研發需求正持續增加，假設光纖雷射被動元件、智慧
照明等營收年增 20%，端面檢測儀與製造設備出貨 10 台。其中，衛星雷射通訊被動
元件營收年增約 50%，營收比重達 60％，預估 2027 年營收 18 億元(YoY+36.1%)，
毛利率 64.3%，EPS 15.35 元。

累計發射數量 2024年 2025年 2026F 2027F 2028F
SpaceX 7,503 10,737 13,036 19,036 26,836
Amazon LEO 2 182 398 698 1,198
One Web 656 656 656 656 656
總計 8,161 11,575 14,090 20,390 28,690
2028 年建立太空資料中心，AIDC LEO 為成長動能 
展望 2028 年，SpaceX 可能於外太空建立約 5GW AI 運算能力，並在軌道部署約 1
萬個 Nvidia GPU 機架。目前 SpaceX 等低軌衛星廠商申請 AIDC 衛星數量已超過 100
萬顆。基於每顆 AIDC 衛星要與前後、左右衛星相連，需配置 6 顆 LCT，較現行衛星
增加 3 顆 LCT，而單顆 LCT 需要 6~９個光纖被動元件(包含合束器、隔離器、波長分
波多工器、濾波器、分光器與端帽等)，因此單顆 AIDC 衛星光纖被動元件用量由 18~27
個增至 36~54 個，帶動萊德光電-KY 產值增為原本的 2 倍至 3 倍。除了 AIDC 衛星
之外，地表的光基地台將以高功率雷射幫助低軌衛星傳遞訊號及能量，解決低軌衛星
太陽能板重量大、供給效率低的問題，並建置太空與地表連接的 6G 寬頻全光網路，
此亦是雷射被動元件營收成長動能。假設 2028 年 SpaceX Starship 發射次數達 130
次，V3 載運總數達 7,800 顆(YoY+21.9%)，光纖雷射被動元件、智慧照明等營收年增
15%至 20%，端面檢測儀與製造設備出貨維持 10 台，在泰國廠營收貢獻提升下，預
估 2028 年營收 23 億元(YoY+27.5%)，毛利率 64.3%，EPS 19.81 元。 
太空雷射對 LEO 發展不可或缺，首次評等給予區間操作，目標價 495 元 
萊德光電-KY 於 2021 年切入衛星雷射通訊領域後，目前在太空雷射元件領域競爭對
手甚少。現階段低軌衛星產業處於發展初期，2026 年至 2030 年衛星發射數量隨著火
箭發射數量增加、運載能力增強而以雙位數 CAGR 成長，將逐年帶動萊德光電-KY 衛
星雷射通訊被動元件等營收走高。參考近期低軌衛星產業 Forward PE 評價落在
15X~40X 區間。以 2028 年 EPS 換算，目前為 23X，位於區間中緣，故首次評等給
予區間操作，目標價 495 元(2028F PE 25X)。

2026.11.23 
梁會鑫 C00255@ibfs.com.tw 
7722.TT LINEPAY 
 累計營收再度創高，惟匯損衝擊獲利增幅收窄

[調升評價]   
   公司簡介 
公司於 2015 年設立，為國內第三方支付龍頭，丁雄注為董事長兼總經理，分別有台
北富邦銀行、聯邦銀行入股，並持有兩間子公司，於 2025 年 7 月經金管會核准設立
電子支付子公司後，已於 2025 年 12 月啟用電子支付。   
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 303 
【前次投資建議】區間操作，303 
【大盤指數/股價】36033 / 225

➢ 前 9 月營收雙位數增長，惟毛利率因營業費用轉列營業成本縮減 
LinePay 累計前 9 月營收為 64.33 億元，YoY+15%，而 LinePay 前 9 月
毛利率為 24.9%，YoY-8.6ppts.，主因電子支付上線後，原本列為營業
費用的管理與研發支出，部分轉列至營業成本所致，惟毛利率亦較 1H26
有所提升，反映電子支付營收缺口逐漸補上，而營業淨利率仍受成本及
費用增長而降至 6.8%，YoY-1.9ppts.，且 3Q26 稅後淨利受業外匯兌損
失影響下較 3Q25 YoY-22.6%下，令整體 2026 年前 9 月稅後淨利僅增
至 4.35 億元，YoY+5.7%， EPS 為 6.12 元。 
➢ 2026 年營收預估將因短期營收出現缺口使年增低於過往水準 
綜觀 4Q26，LinePay 10 月營收為 7.33 億元，YoY+4.1%，占 4Q26 達
成率為 31.6%，營收年增低於過去水準反映短期營收出現缺口所致，考
量前 9 月核心手續費年增維持逾 20%，此外 4Q26 亦有百貨週年慶、電
商節慶與旅遊旺季可望為支付交易量帶來季節性動能，綜合考量之下，
投研部預估 4Q26 營收為 23.17 億元，QoQ+5%，YoY+6.4%，稅後淨
利則為 1.56 億元，QoQ+11.8%，YoY+63.3%，EPS 2.29 元。 
➢ 電支業務有望加速拓展，調升至買進評等 
展望 2026 年，考量 LinePay 與眾多銀行推出多檔活動，預期將可挹注
全年交易量提升，並帶動營收成長，並參考 LinePay 開始施行電子支付
業務，因而投研部預估 2026 年營收為 87.5 億元，YoY+11.2%，此外，
因電子支付業務毛利率較現行手續費毛利率高，預估隨電子支付業務 4
月轉虧為盈後，將有望帶動毛利率逐季改善，因而預估將使毛利率較
2026 年前 9 月增長至 25.2%，YoY-7.5ppts.，預期亦能使淨利有所增
長，稅後淨利則為 5.91 億元，YoY+16.5%，EPS 為 8.58 元。 
綜合前述，以 11/20 收盤價 225 元估算目前評價為 PER 26X，考量過往
本益比區間為 25X~100X，目前評價處於低緣，主要反映資本市場下行，
拖累民眾消費動能縮減所致，考量 2027 年營收仍將持續創高並維持雙
位數增長，並參考電子支付業務用戶擴張至 450 萬人，有望加速帶動電
子支付業務增長，毛利率外溢效果亦將於 2027 年逐步顯現，再加上韓
國、日本等海外市場深化跨境支付，進一步提升 LinePay 獲利能力，因
而投研部調升至買進投資評等，目標價 303 元(2027F PE 30X)。

個股報告

拓展海外市場提升用戶數量及支付據點，公司目標 2026 年交易量突破 1 兆元 
2025 年財務分析中，營收主要來自於手續費收入佔 82%，而金融推廣服務亦佔約 14%，
全年毛利率則為 32.7%，若拆分為細項的話，作為營收主體的手續費收入毛利率約為
20%，金融推廣服務則有 93%。而截至 4Q25，身為國內第三方支付龍頭的 LinePay
用戶數已超過 1,360 萬人、平均每 2 人就有 1 人使用，而用戶綁卡數亦超過 2,100 萬
張，佔全體流通信用卡數逾 1/3，全台支付據點也已成長至 66 萬間，韓國支付據點亦
逾 8 萬間，全球支付據點加總超過 74 萬間，2025 年全年 LinePay 交易額亦達 8,680
億元。 
圖 1：2025 年 LinePay 營收收入及佔比

資料來源：LinePay 
穩定幣逐漸成全球趨勢、台灣政府若開放，LinePay 可望受惠 
隨美國天才法案通過，2025 年全球掀起穩定幣浪潮，美國、香港、新加坡、日本及英
國相繼提升其國內使用穩定幣比例，而穩定幣主要用途有兩項，其一為跨境支付，通過
以穩定幣跨境支付，相較於過往經由銀行結算系統進行跨境支付時，將可節省大量的
手續費成本以及交易時間，因此其為目前主要使用用途，而第二項用途則是提供避險
手段，在阿根廷及其餘新興國家中，其本國貨幣通膨較為嚴重，民眾普遍不信任政府發
行之法幣，而穩定幣則在其中提供穩定的美元避險功能，因此穩定幣亦於新興國家中
大幅使用。而考量 LinePay 具有足夠科技實力建立穩定幣平台並發行之，因而雖台灣
監管力道趨嚴，但台灣政府若開放，預期 LinePay 可望受惠而持續具成長空間。

2026 年前 9 月營收維持雙位數增長，惟毛利率因營業費用轉列營業成本而縮減 
LinePay 2026 年累計前 9 月營收為 64.33 億元，YoY+15%，而 LinePay 前 9 月毛利
率為 24.9%，YoY-8.6ppts.，主因電子支付上線後，原本列為營業費用的管理與研發支
出，部分轉列至營業成本所致，惟毛利率亦較 1H26 有所提升，反映電子支付營收缺
口逐漸補上，此外由於 LinePay 2025 年申請電支執照及持續投入 IDC，使其研發費用
持續維持高點，以及行銷及管理費用亦有所增長，預期在電支平台建置完成及部分營
業費用轉列至營業成本後，整體營業費用率下降至 18%，YoY-6.8ppts.，惟營業淨利
率仍受成本及費用增長而降至 6.8%，YoY-1.9ppts.，且 3Q26 稅後淨利受業外匯兌損
失影響下較 3Q25 YoY-22.6%下，令整體 2026 年前 9 月稅後淨利僅增至 4.35 億元，
YoY+5.7%，EPS 為 6.12 元。 
2026 年營收預估將因短期營收出現缺口使年增低於過往水準 
綜觀 4Q26，LinePay 10 月營收為 7.33 億元，YoY+4.1%，占 4Q26 達成率為 31.6%，
約略符合投研部預期，營收年增低於過去水準主要反映 2025 年 12 月起轉入自營電子
支付，結束部分合作合約，短期營收出現缺口所致，考量前 9 月核心手續費年增維持
逾 20%，再加上電子支付業務已於 4 月轉虧為盈，此外 4Q26 亦有百貨週年慶、電商
節慶與旅遊旺季可望為支付交易量帶來季節性動能，因而投研部預估其營收成長幅度
雖將較 2025 年收窄，惟 4Q26 營收及獲利有望改善，綜合考量之下，投研部預估 4Q26
營收為 23.17 億元，按季節成長率來看 QoQ+5%，YoY+6.4%，稅後淨利則為 1.56 億
元，QoQ+11.8%，YoY+63.3%，EPS 2.29 元。 
電支業務有望加速拓展，調升至買進評等，目標價 303 元 
展望 2026 年，考量公司與眾多銀行推出多檔活動，預期將可挹注全年交易量提升，並
帶動營收成長，並參考 LinePay 開始施行電子支付業務，向店家收取之手續費收入不
必再與 iPASS MONEY 拆分收益，預期亦將挹注營收，惟參考 2025 年 12 月起轉入自
營電子支付，結束部分合作合約，短期營收出現缺口，所幸隨乘車碼、好友推薦、未成
年帳戶等多元場景擴大用戶規模，帶動電子支付用戶擴張至 450 萬人，並有望加速帶
動電子支付業務增長，綜合考量下，投研部預估 2026 年營收為 87.5 億元，YoY+11.2%，
此外，因電子支付業務毛利率較現行手續費毛利率高，預估隨電子支付業務 4 月轉虧
為盈後，將有望帶動毛利率逐季改善，因而預估將使毛利率較 2026 年前 9 月增長至
25.2%，YoY-7.5ppts.，且 LinePay 與眾多銀行皆有合作關係，在推出電子錢包服務
後，亦可與部分不願意使用信用卡之店家進行不同模式的合作，而隨著電支業務上線，

### Page 4

- 4 -

個股報告 
預期研發費用將有所縮減，再加上電子支付部分，銀行將負擔大部分行銷費用，綜合考
量下也將節省部分營業費用，預期亦能使淨利有所增長，稅後淨利則為 5.91 億元，
YoY+16.5%，EPS 為 8.58 元。 
2027 年部分，則預期在電子支付業務成長穩定，再加上海外獲利增長而使整體營收及
稅後淨利再度增長，投研部預估 LinePay 2027 年營收為 103.54 億元，YoY+18.3%，
稅後淨利則為 6.86 億元，YoY+16%，EPS 10.09 元。 
綜合前述，以 11/20 收盤價 225 元估算目前評價約為 PER 26X，考量 LinePay 過往
本益比區間為 25X~100X，目前評價處於低緣，主要反映資本市場下行，拖累民眾消
費動能縮減所致，考量 LinePay 2027 年營收仍將持續創高並維持雙位數增長，且非
現金支付仍有成長空間，再加上電子支付業務挹注後，整體成長潛力仍具前景，並考
量 Line pay money 電子支付公司運用乘車碼、好友推薦、未成年帳戶等多元活動擴
大用戶規模，帶動電子支付用戶擴張至 450 萬人，並有望加速帶動電子支付業務增
長，毛利率提升之外溢效果亦將於 2027 年逐步顯現，再加上韓國、日本等海外市場
亦透過高額回饋與點數活動深化跨境支付，進一步提升 LinePay 獲利能力，因而投研
部調升至買進投資評等，目標價 303 元(2027F PE 30X)。

Document 290: An RBA peak of greater conviction (2026-11-27)

Source PDF: all report pdf/An RBA peak of greater conviction (2026-11-27).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #rba
An RBA peak of greater conviction
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
08:56 November 13 2026 Share
We already believed the RBA cash rate was likely at a peak, even if RBA officials were less
convinced. A deeper housing downturn and early signs of cracks in the consumer sector
point to an even softer 2027. Fading inflation risks from oil also boost our conviction on
rates being at a peak.

### Page 2

It was already our view that the RBA was likely done hiking the cash rate after the November
increase. Since then, our conviction on that call has strengthened. Later today we will publish
the November Market Outlook with revised forecasts. GDP growth, the labour market and housing
market have all been revised down in 2027. Underlying inflation has been revised down slightly in the
second half of 2027, bringing the year-ended figure for calendar 2027 more decisively inside the

RBA’s 2–3% target range than our previous forecast round. The unemployment rate forecast has
been revised up to peak above 5%. Based on this revised forecast profile, an increase in the cash
rate from here would be unlikely and – we would argue – undesirable.
A range of considerations underlie this forecast revision. The housing market has undershot our
forecasts over the past couple of months. Turnover and financing activity have been coming in
under our earlier expectations. Mortgage loan inquiries have taken another leg down in October after

the September rate hike, after finding something of a level in prior months, with the additional impact
of the November rate hike still to come.
We are also seeing early signs of a turn in the consumer. Consumer income and spending have
so far been quite resilient to pressures from fuel prices and rising interest rates. But our Westpac
Card Tracker is starting to show that discretionary services spending is turning down again in Q4,
joining the discretionary goods category that was already quite weak. We would not over-emphasise

this point, but it does imply that the risks of further surprising resilience are fading.
Subsequent to the November Monetary Policy Board (MPB) meeting, business survey data
has shown weaker conditions and a sharp decline in forward orders. They also show further
evidence that current inflation is mostly a non-labour cost shock that is not being fully
passed through to consumer prices.
As we noted following the November MPB meeting, oil and fuel prices are already materially

lower than the track assumed in the RBA’s forecasts. Dated Brent is nearly one-fourth lower than
on 28 October, the date the RBA’s forecasts were finalised, and spot Brent is around 15% lower. This
significantly lowers the risk of a new wave of pass-through of fuel costs to other prices, as well as
easing consumer inflation perceptions and expectations.
In the media conference following the November meeting, Governor Bullock flagged that it was a

good time to “take stock” of the policy tightening to date. Both we and the market read this as a
signal that the MPB would pause at the December meeting. The 6:3 split vote at the meeting also
suggests that some external members are more attentive to downside risks than the internal
members.
A rate hike in February 2027 cannot be ruled out but it is looking even less likely given signs
in the data and surveys. The MPB will at least discuss a hike in February. The staff and senior

officials remain sensitive to inflation risks and have a downbeat view of supply capacity growth.
Given what we are seeing in the economy now, though, a rate hike in 2027 would require
either a material new inflation shock that has not yet occurred, or would constitute a policy
error on the part of the RBA (something that we will never forecast as a base case, knowing the
talents and capabilities of the staff and the Board). We concur with the dissenting voters in the MPB

that the downside risks need more attention. Policy is clearly restrictive, and the risk of overdoing the
inflation correction is growing. And by the time we get to February, we think there will be more
evidence that the domestic economy is slowing materially, and the inflation outlook with it.

Document 318: CEE & CCA week ahead_ Rate decisions in Poland, Kazakhstan to accompany GDP and inflation data

Source PDF: all report pdf/CEE & CCA week ahead_ Rate decisions in Poland, Kazakhstan to accompany GDP and inflation data.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

The National Bank of Poland is expected to keep rates
unchanged, while Kazakhstan faces a close rate decision. GDP
and inﬂation data releases will also make for a busy week
across the region

Adam Antoniak, Peter Virovacz, Muhammet Mercan and Dmitry
Dolgin
We're expecting the National Bank of Poland to keep rates on hold on 2
December
Poland: NBP expected to keep rates
unchanged
On Monday, ﬂash November CPI is likely to show that consumer
inﬂation moderated slightly in Poland, as oil prices and,
consequently, retail petrol prices have been falling in recent weeks.
We estimate that food prices increased after ﬁve consecutive
months of year-on-year declines, while core inﬂation rose back
above 3%, mainly due to a low reference base from November
2025, when core prices declined by 0.1% month-on-month.
Your privacy is important

Save preferences Accept all

### Page 2

Also on Monday, the 3Q26 GDP composition should show
somewhat slower consumption growth amid moderating real wage
growth and pressure from high petrol prices. Meanwhile, ﬁxed
investment likely remained robust, as projects ﬁnanced under the
Domestic Recovery and Resilience Programme were in full swing.
The contribution from net exports was probably weaker than the
0.5ppt reported in 2Q26.
On Wednesday, the National Bank of Poland should keep interest

rates on hold, with the main policy rate remaining at 3.75%. Polish
monetary authorities usually refrain from changing interest rates in
December unless there is an urgent need for a policy adjustment.
Core inﬂation was 0.0% MoM in each of the last two months,
suggesting no indirect impact from higher fuel prices on broader
inﬂation. The government is preparing measures to freeze
household gas and electricity bills in 2027, while oil prices have
moderated. CPI inﬂation is therefore likely to be lower than

presented in the central bank’s November projection.
Hungary: Third-quarter growth likely to
have been sluggish
Hungary's detailed GDP breakdown is due on Tuesday and should
conﬁrm sluggish third-quarter growth. Net exports could surprise
on the positive side, while investment activity was probably the
main drag and consumption the key growth engine. On the
production side, a sharp fall in agriculture and weakness in
construction should be oﬀset by industry and services. A minor

upward revision to the ﬂash estimate is also on the cards.
Thursday brings the November budget data. So far, so good for this
year’s budget execution. Another manageable monthly deﬁcit in
November should be followed by a signiﬁcant improvement in
December as EU funds ﬂow in. The revised full-year target therefore
looks achievable, with the Maastricht deﬁcit potentially even
outperforming the 7.5% of GDP plan.
Turkey: Third-quarter growth expected at

2.9%
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.
You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.
Essential
Performance Cookies
Targeting Cookies

### Page 3

Third-quarter GDP data is due on Monday. We expect year-on-year
growth of 2.9%, on the back of resilient services and a relative
recovery in the construction sector despite sluggish performance in
the industry. While early indicators implied a further softening in
4Q26 domestic demand, we currently see full-year GDP growth at
2.8%, with risks tilted to the downside.
November CPI follows on Thursday, when we expect inﬂation to
come in at 0.8% MoM and 29.7% YoY, versus 29.8% a month ago,

supported by the recent decline in energy prices. Risks are on the
upside given pressure on food prices.
CCA: Armenia inﬂation and Kazakhstan's
rate decision
Armenia's November CPI is due on Thursday. We expect November
inﬂation to accelerate noticeably from 4.3% to 4.7% YoY, partly due
to adverse base eﬀects, and partly reﬂecting a genuine increase in
price pressures. Such a print would strengthen our expectation that
the Central Bank of Armenia will deliver another rate hike in

December, following its recent shift towards tighter policy. The
composition will also be important: food inﬂation moderated in
October, while housing and services pressures strengthened despite
the continued support from a strong dram and a somewhat more
restrained ﬁscal policy. A further broadening of domestic price
pressures would provide a strong argument for another rate
increase.
Kazakhstan's base rate decision comes on Friday. We expect the

National Bank of Kazakhstan to cut the base rate by 25bp to
15.50%, although we see this as a relatively low-conviction call,
with a hold the main alternative. Further easing would be
supported by the broader disinﬂation trend, some moderation in
inﬂation expectations and still-high real rates. At the same time,
sticky services inﬂation, a less supportive tenge and persistent
ﬁscal and tariﬀ-related risks argue for caution, while the NBK
previously signalled a low probability of another rate move before

year-end.

実質ＧＤＰ成長率の推移
民間最終消費支出 民間企業設備 その他民需 公需 外需
（資料）内閣府経済社会総合研究所「四半期別ＧＤＰ速報」
( )内は年率換算値

１. 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、前期比▲0.0％（年率▲0.1％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナ
ス成長となった（当研究所予測 10月 30日：前期比▲0.1％、年率▲0.3％）。 
２. 物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下や天候不順の影響で民間消費が前期比
▲0.3％の減少となったことがマイナス成長の主因である。一方、高水準の企業収益を背
景に設備投資は前期比 1.6％の高い伸びとなった。 
３. 外需寄与度は前期比▲0.6％（前期比年率▲2.4％）と 3四半期ぶりのマイナスとなった。
財貨・サービスの輸出が前期比▲0.2％と減少する一方、代替調達の進展に伴う原油輸入
量の急増を主因として財貨・サービスの輸入が同 2.5％の増加となった。 
４. 原油の代替調達に伴う輸入増加による成長率の押し下げ（前期比寄与度▲0.5％）は、国
家備蓄の放出がほぼなくなったことによる公的在庫変動のプラス寄与（同 0.5％）によっ
てほぼ相殺されている。両者を合わせた成長率への影響はほぼ中立となった。 
５. マイナス成長の主因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響が大きい
が、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が高い。
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むこ
となどから、前期比年率 0％台半ばとなり、マイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにと
どまると予想している。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-11-16

となった。輸入デフレーターが前期比 3.3％となり、輸出デフレーターの伸び（同 1.3％）を上回っ
たことが GDPデフレーターを押し下げたが、国内需要デフレーターが前期比 1.0％（4-6月期：同
1.4％）の上昇となった。

### Page 3

＜需要項目別の動き＞ 
民間消費は、物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下や天候不順の影響で、前期比▲0.3％と
2四半期連続で減少した。2026年 4-6月期が前期比 0.0％から同▲0.3％へ下方修正されたため、民
間消費の減少は 2四半期連続となった。 
3月末の自動車税の環境性能割廃止に伴う自動車販売の大幅増加や、2027年 4月の省エネ基準引
き上げを見越した駆け込み需要によるエアコン販売の好調により、4-6月期に大幅に増加した耐久
財が、その反動で大きく落ち込んだことも消費の下押し要因となった。 
実質家計消費の内訳を形態別にみると、外食、旅行、宿泊などのサービスは前期比 0.1％と小幅
な増加となったが、自動車、エアコンなどの耐久財が前期比▲3.5％の減少となったほか、値上げが
続く食料品などの非耐久財が同▲0.2％、被服・履物、家具などの半耐久財が同▲1.4％の減少とな
った。 
名目雇用者報酬は前年比 5.5％と、4-6月期の同 5.3％から伸びが高まった。実質雇用者報酬は前
年比 2.5％（4-6月期：同 2.6％）と 10四半期連続で増加した。

住宅投資は前期比▲1.1％と 2四半期連続で減少した。新設住宅着工戸数（季節調整済・年率換算
値）は、建築物省エネ法・建築基準法改正前の駆け込み需要の反動で、2025年 1-3月期の 86.7万
戸から 4-6月期に 62.0万戸へ急減した後、7-9月が 71.9万戸と持ち直し、その後は概ね 70万戸台
半ばで推移している。GDP統計の住宅投資は住宅価格上昇の影響もあり弱めの動きとなっている。

設備投資は前期比 1.6％と 3四半期ぶりに増加した。設備投資が高い伸びとなった一因は、4-6月
期に特許権の海外譲渡に伴う研究開発投資の減少によって落ち込んだ反動である。ただ、その影響
を除いても、高水準の企業収益を背景に、人手不足に対応した省力化投資、デジタル化に向けた情
報関連投資、AI・半導体関連などの成長分野への投資、Eコマースの拡大に伴う建設投資などを中
心に、回復基調が続いている。

公的需要は前期比 1.8％と 2四半期ぶりに増加した。高校授業料無償化や公立小学校の給食費無
償化に伴う家計消費支出の減少分が計上され、4-6月期に前期比 1.5％の高い伸びとなった政府消費
がその反動で同▲0.0％と減少し、公的固定資本形成は前期比▲1.1％と 2四半期連続で減少した。
一方、原油輸入量の急減を受けた国家備蓄原油の放出により前期比・寄与度▲0.5％の大幅マイナス
となった公的在庫変動が、備蓄の放出がほぼなくなったことで、プラス寄与（同 0.5％）に転じた
ため、公的需要全体では前期比 1.8％の増加となった。

外需寄与度は前期比▲0.6％（前期比年率▲2.4％）と 3四半期ぶりのマイナスとなった。財貨・
サービスの輸出は前期比▲0.2％と 4四半期ぶりに減少した。半導体製造装置などの資本財輸出が好
調だったことから、財輸出は同 2.4％と増加したが、4-6月期に大幅に増加した研究開発サービスの

### Page 4

（2026年度後半は低空飛行が続く見通し） 
2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の主因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響
が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が
高い。消費者物価上昇率は 2026年 9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となったが、原材料価格の上昇を耐久
財や日用品などの販売価格に転嫁する動きが今後さらに広がることから、2026年度末にかけて 3％
台まで高まる公算が大きい。物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下を背景に 2026年度後半
の民間消費は弱めの動きが続くことが予想される。 
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むことなどか
ら、前期比年率 0％台半ばとなり、マイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまると予想してい
る。続く 2027年 1-3月期は、物価上昇率が 3％台まで高まることに加え、飲食料品の消費税率引き
下げを控えた買い控えも影響し、民間消費が減少する可能性が高く、実質 GDP成長率は前期比年
率 0％台前半にとどまるだろう。2026年度後半の日本経済は物価高による民間消費の低迷を主因と
して、低空飛行が続くことが予想される。

また、本資料は情報提供が目的であり、記載の意見や予測は、いかなる契約の締結や解約を勧誘するものではありません。

Document 411: Sovereign debt_ When Short-Term Shocks and Shifts in Debt Structure Leave a Lasting Fiscal Burden — The Case of Brazil (2026-11-12)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Sovereign debt_ When Short-Term Shocks and Shifts in Debt Structure Leave a Lasting Fiscal Burden — The Case of Brazil (2026-11-12).pdf

### Page 1

10 November 2026
The bank
for a changing

SOVEREIGN DEBT: WHEN SHORT-TERM SHOCKS AND SHIFTS IN DEBT STRUCTURE LEAVE A LASTING FISCAL
BURDEN — THE CASE OF BRAZIL
Salim Hammad
Brazil’s recent post-election yield shock risks leaving a lasting imprint on public finances — compounded by already high interest rates and a
changing debt structure. Large refinancing needs and a rising share of floating-rate debt will transmit rapidly and significantly higher interest

rates to the debt service through distinct but reinforcing channels.
SOURCES : MACROBOND, BCB
Brazil’s presidential elections triggered a sharp repricing of Brazil’s rate outlook

10-year government bond yields
SELIC - Policy Rate%
CHART 1
An abrupt post-election market awakening

In the wake of the 2nd round of the general elections in late October, Brazilian markets — which had expected a Flavio Bolsonaro
victory — sold off sharply after Luiz Inacio Lula da Silva won a fourth presidential term. Both the equity and FX markets
experienced large intraday drops (14% of the Ibovespa stock index and -11% BRL vs. USD). Meanwhile, the yield on Brazil’s 10-year
government bond rose 250 basis points to 15.5%, reaching its highest level in over a decade (Chart 1) as investors worried about

the fiscal impact of costly campaign promises amidst large and persistent fiscal imbalances.
Fiscal risks have reshaped Brazil’s public debt structure, magnifying the cost of persistently high interest rates
Absent a confidence-infusing fiscal adjustment, Brazil’s interest bill is unlikely to decline in the short term, maintaining upward
pressure on public debt (83% of GDP in October 2026) and limiting budgetary leeway.
Three transmission channels, amplified by changing features of the debt structure, explain this persistence.

The first channel: The rise in long-term yields will increase rollover costs.
The sovereign must refinance roughly 20% of its debt stock each year. An average maturity of 4.1 years spreads refinancing over
time but does not shield the interest bill from a sustained rise in borrowing costs. As maturing securities are replaced, higher
issuance rates progressively lift the average borrowing cost — even for fixed-rate debt, whose interest costs remain unchanged

until refinancing. A sustained yield shock therefore feeds into debt servicing as the stock rolls over.

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 10 November 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
The second channel: Demand for floating-rate instruments will strengthen until confidence in fiscal policy improves.
In turn, a larger share of SELIC-linked debt heightens the debt stock’s sensitivity to the prevailing high policy rate. Already SELIC-linked
securities reached a record 52.6% of the debt stock in August 2026, around 12 percentage points above their share at the start of President

Lula’s last term, versus a low of 18.4% in September 2014 (Chart 2). Weaker demand for fixed-rate securities with maturities exceeding five
years, amid increased fiscal uncertainty, has encouraged this shift1.
The third channel: The BCB may delay rate cuts until fiscal policy over the next mandate is better defined.
A slower easing cycle would prolong high interest costs on SELIC-linked debt, whose servicing costs adjust with the policy rate without

waiting for refinancing. With these securities accounting for a growing share of the debt stock, delayed monetary easing would postpone
relief for the Treasury’s interest bill — even if long-term market yields subsequently ease.
These channels operate at different speeds: a rise in long-term yields increases rollover costs, whereas floating-rate debt reprices with
the SELIC rate, not automatically with 10-year yields. Meanwhile, a slower monetary easing cycle and stronger demand for floating-rate

securities compound pressure on the interest bill.
The fiscal–monetary policy nexus is therefore likely to remain a source of strain on Brazil’s public finances: fiscal uncertainty could delay
monetary easing, while greater floating-rate exposure would prolong the burden of high policy rates on the interest bill.
salim.hammad@bnpparibas.com
1 Fiscal slippage and uncertainty make investors less willing to lock in nominal returns over long horizons: if inflation or market yields rise, the purchasing power of fixed payments

declines or the bonds’ market value falls. Investors therefore demand higher yields to bear this risk, making long-term fixed-rate funding more expensive for the Treasury. By issuing
floating-rate bonds instead, the Treasury offers returns that adjust with the SELIC rate, sustaining demand, securing financing and reducing the risk of failed auctions. However
longer floating-rate maturities may reduce rollover risk, but do not remove the repricing exposure. Floating-rate issuance helps secure financing but transfers interest-rate risk to

the Treasury.

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Reserve Bank of India

India’s domestic demand cycle remained 
resilient in the second quarter, with 
consumption, public capex and 
manufacturing providing a solid base 
despite external shocks. Industrial 
activity was strong spanning consumer 
durables, capital goods and infra-related 
output. Service-sector indicators, 
including credit growth and fiscal 
support (revenue spending was higher 
than capex growth), were supportive 
despite the partial pass-through of 
higher input costs. Exports were firm, 
even as the contribution from net 
exports is likely to moderate due to a 
firmer import bill. Q2FY27 real GDP 
growth is expected at around 7.7% yoy, 
compared with 7.8% in Q1. For FY27, we 
see upside risks to our annual GDP 
growth. Some moderation is, 
nonetheless, likely in the second half as 
tighter policy conditions, high energy 
prices, sub-normal monsoon and indirect 
tax-driven base effects come into play.

We expect the RBI to retain its 
calibrated-tightening stance, with 
another rate hike likely at the December 
meeting. Our base case is for a 25bp 
increase, although a larger, front-loaded 
move cannot be completely ruled out. 
The case for further tightening has 
strengthened following the renewed rise 
in inflation. CPI inflation rose to 6.1% yoy 
in October, breaching the upper end of 
the RBI’s tolerance band, while food 
inflation accelerated to 8.5%. Core/ 
core-core inflation and diffusion indices 
have also firmed, suggesting that price 
pressures are becoming more broad-
based rather than being confined to food. 
The RBI is likely to weigh this inflation 
trajectory against still-resilient domestic 
growth. Oil, food prices due to poor 
weather conditions, and evolution of 
underlying inflation will remain 
important variables.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

South Korea

November trade and CPI data are likely 
to underscore steady growth and sticky 
inflation despite recent stock market 
volatility fuelled by concerns over the AI 
outlook. Export growth is expected to 
remain strong at around 90% yoy, versus 
93.9% in the previous month, supported 
by increases of over 200% in 
semiconductor shipments and over 400% 
in computer shipments. CPI inflation is 
expected to rise to 3.0% yoy in 
November from 2.8% in the previous 
month, as accumulated imported cost 
pressures pass through to domestic 
goods and services prices.

Thailand

We expect Thailand’s headline inflation 
to ease to 3.6% yoy in November 2026 
from 3.9% yoy in October. The 
moderation largely reflected easing 
energy price pressures, and softer food 
inflation, although headline inflation 
remained elevated above the central 
bank’s 1-3% target range. The November 
print is unlikely to alter the near-term

### Page 3

The Week Ahead November 27, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

monetary policy outlook, with the policy 
rate likely to remain steady for the 
remainder of 2026.

Vietnam

We expect Vietnam’s economic 
momentum to remain firm in November 
2026, with goods exports growing by 
55.0% yoy, up from 51.4% in October 
2026, reflecting favourable base effects 
and sustained electronics demand amid 
a strong global technology cycle. Retail 
sales growth should edge up to 13.6% 
yoy year-to-date, supported by resilient 
household and tourism spending. 
Meanwhile, we anticipate headline 
inflation to ease to 4.5% yoy, from 5.1%, 
as transport price pressures moderate, 
with domestic fuel prices declining 
alongside global crude oil prices.

Hong Kong SAR

Retail sales growth is expected to 
moderate slightly from 6.4% yoy in 
September to 6.3% in October, as 
tourism support softened. Growth in 
daily Mainland visitor arrivals slowed 
sharply from 29.0% yoy to 15.0%, while 
resident departures moderated from 18.1% 
to 10.3%. Consumption indicators 
remained subdued, with both the retail 
and restaurant diffusion indices 
weakening further. Despite headline CPI 
inflation of 2.2%, basic food prices fell 
0.8% yoy and clothing & apparel prices 
rose only 0.5%, suggesting underlying 
consumer demand remained soft.

利差優勢消長，新興與商品
貨幣走勢各異
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
高油價、美元偏強及全球景氣分化持續牽動新興與
商品貨幣。澳洲就業轉弱、通膨改善，澳洲央行升
息週期料暫告一段落，澳幣短線料區間盤整。紐西
蘭央行意外按兵不動削弱紐幣短期動能，但升息預
期仍提供支撐。加拿大央行政策轉鷹及高油價利好
加幣，惟經濟與貿易不確定性限制升值空間。中國
內需偏弱，但出口暢旺、企業結匯需求強，加上政
策對升值容忍度提高，人民幣料溫和升值。南非幣
受美元、高油價及利差收窄壓抑，但財政紀律改善、
信用前景轉佳及實質利率優勢有助緩和貶值壓力，
料維持區間震盪。
市場投資展望
資產 4Q26看法 投資展望

紐幣 NZDUSD 0.59 美元強勢壓抑紐元表現，惟中期復甦動能仍在
加幣 USDCAD 1.39 央行轉鷹支撐加幣，惟經濟及貿易風險限制升值空間

《投資關鍵 – 澳洲景氣放緩、澳央轉向觀望，預期
澳幣盤整》
隨著國際油價維持高位，澳洲央行（RBA）於9月再
度升息將現金利率提高至4.6%，官員雖表示若通膨
壓力持續，不排除進一步緊縮。
但隨著高利率與高通膨壓力持續拖累經濟動能，近期
公布的9月就業意外減少3.93萬人、失業率更升至
4.7%，已連續兩個月落在澳央主席認為可有效壓抑
通膨的失業率4.5~5.0%區間；通膨方面，9月份澳洲
CPI雖由4.0%升至4.3%，但核心CPI僅有2.2%，加以
住房通膨明顯回落，因此本行預期澳央轉向觀望、本
輪升息暫時告終，2027年政策轉向觀望，如國際油
價回落至90美元以下，澳央有降息空間。
外需方面，中國經濟基本面持續低迷，消費與投資不

振亦難以挹注澳洲出口動能，外需對澳幣支撐力量有
限，加以預期聯準會在12月再度升息，將呈現「利
差有撐但優勢遞減」格局，因此預估澳幣短線維持在
0.7~0.71區間盤整，要進一步突破或要待2Q27美國
通膨回落、降息預期再度升溫，方有走揚力量。
重點摘要
• 經濟基本面降溫，核心CPI穩
定，澳洲央行暫停升息轉向
觀望，澳幣維持在0.7~0.71
中性盤整。

紐西蘭通膨升溫訊號浮現，月度物價年增 5.2%
《投資關鍵 - 央行意外按兵不動拖累紐元，後續升息
預期提供下檔支撐》
過去一個月，受美元強勢、美債殖利率及油價走高拖
累，紐幣兌美元一度下探0.5565，其後隨升息預期升
溫而回穩。惟紐西蘭央行10月28日意外維持OCR於
2.75%不變，促使市場下修短期升息預期，紐幣再度
承壓。
紐西蘭經濟延續溫和復甦，企業信心及農產品價格改
善，惟勞動市場仍有閒置產能。雖然整體通膨低於預
期，但核心通膨及價格廣度上升，加上油價高企與紐

幣偏弱，我們預期4Q26通膨仍具黏性，價格壓力擴散
風險猶存。
我們仍維持偏鷹派的政策基準情境。10月以4比3票按
兵不動，較反映升息時點後移，而非整體政策路徑轉
向，緊縮週期尚未結束。我們仍預期12月及明年3月具
升息空間，尤其若通膨、油價及匯率壓力持續，政策
立場料維持偏緊。
展望4Q26，10月意外按兵不動削弱紐幣利差支撐，我
們仍看好紐元中期修復空間，惟短期上行動能有所減
弱。升息預期降溫，加上大選、美元及全球風險情緒
等不確定性，料使匯價維持較高波動。進入1Q27後，
隨政策不確定性逐步下降，經濟溫和復甦有望成為紐
幣主要支撐。考量匯價已反映相當程度的成長疑慮及
外部利空，若基本面持續改善，紐幣中期仍具修復空
間。
• 展望4Q26，10月意外按兵不動
削弱紐幣利差支撐，我們仍看
好紐元中期修復空間，惟短期
上行動能有所減弱。
• 隨政策不確定性逐步下降，經

度的成長疑慮及外部利空，若
基本面持續改善，紐幣中期仍
具修復空間。

### Page 4

通膨數據回落，6月降息的可能性加大重點摘要 《投資關鍵 - 央行轉鷹支撐加幣，惟經濟及貿易風險
限制升值空間》
能源價格高企令加拿大通膨面臨上行風險，加拿大央
行政策預期轉鷹亦推動加美利差劣勢收窄，支持加幣
自10月中旬低點反彈。不過，隨本次25個基點升息
已大致反映於市場定價，加幣能否延續升勢將更取決
於央行對後續緊縮路徑的指引，以及市場對終端利率
的重新定價，而非本次升息本身。
加拿大央行最新升息25個基點至2.5%，為逾三年來
首次升息，並表示若能源價格進一步推升廣泛通膨壓
力，不排除繼續收緊政策。央行亦將2027年通膨預
測由2.0%上調至3.1%，並將今年經濟成長預測由
0.7%上調至1.0%，短期政策訊號偏鷹。惟核心通膨
仍相對溫和，勞動市場、製造業需求及招聘意願亦呈
轉弱跡象，意味若經濟活動進一步降溫，持續升息空
間或有限。高油價及偏高的投機性加幣空頭部位仍構
成短期利好，但美加貿易不確定性可能繼續拖累企業
投資及成長前景。因此，我們維持4Q26至1Q27加幣
中性看法。短期加美利差收窄及油價高企有望限制加

幣下行風險，但在增長前景改善或央行釋出更明確的
持續緊縮訊號前，加幣進一步升值空間有限。
